# 417: Urshifu Enters the Battlefield!!

Galar Region, Isle of Armor.

"Master, Johan's Kubfu evolved." Leon's voice came through the phone.

"I saw it, little Leon." Mustard replied, holding his phone in one hand and a bag of chips in the other while lounging on the sofa, watching the live broadcast.

He was clearly thrilled that Kubfu had evolved.

Maybe now, that little guy wouldn't need to come back to the Isle of Armor?

"But Master, didn't you say Kubfu needed to clear the Tower of Two Fists to evolve?" Leon asked, confused.

Mustard chuckled, "The Tower of Two Fists is just a test of their martial prowess. That little rascal… already climbed it."

"It already did?" Leon was stunned.

"Yep."

Back then, Kubfu had stormed the Tower of Darkness in the middle of the night and read the Scroll of Darkness—but didn't evolve.

Then, the next morning, it ran over to the Tower of Waters and read the Scroll of Waters.

After reading both scrolls, it still didn't evolve.

Instead, it learned both Single Strike Style and Rapid Strike Style techniques.

Other Kubfu would accumulate enough experience, then read the scroll to grasp their chosen technique.

But this one? It flipped the script entirely.

It mastered the techniques first—then realized it hadn't built up enough energy to evolve.

Of course, compared to other Kubfu, its progress wasn't actually slow. It was just that every Kubfu had different "capacities" for growth.

"So, did it evolve into a Single Strike Urshifu or a Rapid Strike Urshifu?" Leon asked.

"Well, even this old man doesn't know." Mustard's voice carried a hint of mischief.

"Huh?"

Mustard's tone became slightly more serious. "Listen, little Leon, don't get too caught up in labels. An Urshifu's form depends on the techniques it has mastered. But if it has mastered both…"

Leon took a sharp breath.

In the end, an Urshifu's style was determined by the techniques it wielded.

Whether Single Strike or Rapid Strike, their base stats were the same.

If it had mastered both techniques…

Could it switch freely?

Crunch, crunch—

Mustard popped another handful of chips into his mouth. "Come on, little Leon, let's watch the match together."

"Well, I'm watching from Charizard's back, so…"

Back to the battlefield.

Kubfu's evolution into Urshifu had set the entire stadium ablaze with excitement.

Especially after witnessing the incredibly stylish way it evolved—calmly walking out of the flames as if they were nothing—the crowd erupted in thunderous cheers.

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu struck a fighting stance, standing strong and energized.

Urshifu enters the battlefield!!

If Kotetsu from the anime could keep regaining stamina through evolution, why couldn't Urshifu recover by evolving too?

Kabu's expression was more serious than ever.

He admired Johan and Kubfu's relentless fighting spirit and respected their dedication to battle.

But admiration aside… he was still their opponent.

The pressure from Urshifu wasn't just because it had recovered—it was because the Urshifu standing before him was far stronger than before.

Not only had Urshifu fully recovered from burns and reset its lowered defenses, but its sheer presence alone was overwhelming.

"Centiskorch, Will-O-Wisp again!"

Centiskorch, too, could feel Urshifu's immense pressure.

But it didn't hesitate at Kabu's command.

Three fireballs shot toward Urshifu at high speed.

Johan and Urshifu both smiled slightly.

Urshifu's stance shifted.

It raised both hands and lifted one foot slightly, its expression focused and serious.

"Here it comes," Johan murmured.

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu let out a low roar and punched forward at the incoming fireballs.

Splash—

For a brief moment, it sounded like rushing water.

As Urshifu's fist struck the fireballs, a deep blue aura of water surrounded its fist.

A punch, a side kick, a spin, another punch!

With smooth, flowing movements, Urshifu shattered all three fireballs.

Then Johan commanded:

"Aqua Jet!"

Splash—

This time, the sound of water was even clearer. Water flowed around Urshifu's body, enveloping it as it charged toward Centiskorch at high speed.

Fast!

Kabu's heart skipped a beat. "Centiskorch, Burn Up!"

[Burn Up: A powerful Fire-type attack that burns away the user's flames, temporarily removing its Fire typing after use.]

Clearly, Kabu wasn't entirely sure about Urshifu's new abilities.

But from their brief exchange, he suspected that this Urshifu might now have a Water typing.

Burn Up would not only deliver a powerful strike—it would eliminate Centiskorch's Fire weakness to Water attacks.

However…

BAM!!

Before Centiskorch could unleash Burn Up, Urshifu had already closed the distance with Aqua Jet!

Johan swung his arm. "Surging Strikes!"

Rapid Strike Style Secret Technique: Surging Strikes!

A move that guarantees a critical hit!

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu's sharp eyes flashed as it unleashed a flurry of rapid punches, each surrounded by spiraling water.

As Urshifu's fists struck Centiskorch's burning body, steam rose into the air.

BAM! BAM! BAM!!

Three heavy impacts.

"Fffuuu!!"

With a final cry, Centiskorch crashed onto the ground—unable to use Burn Up in time.

The entire arena fell silent.

Until the referee announced: "C-Centiskorch is unable to battle!"

"Whoa!!"

The audience erupted in shock.

"A-A one-shot?!"

"Crap, he's actually sweeping the whole team!"

"Water-type moves?! Did Kubfu gain the Water typing after evolving?"

With a Water typing and Water-type attacks, Kabu's situation had changed drastically.

As a Fire-type specialist, he had clear weaknesses—Fire is weak to Ground, Rock, and Water.

His team did include countermeasures—like Scovillain, a Grass/Fire Pokémon that resisted all three of those weaknesses while benefiting from the sun.

But Scovillain had already been defeated.

Kabu had studied Johan's team and knew Johan didn't usually use Water, Ground, or Rock-type Pokémon.

He just hadn't accounted for Kubfu evolving.

Who would have?!

"This is a Rapid Strike Urshifu—Fighting and Water! Fire-types are in trouble now!"** Lucian said, sneaking glances at Flint.

Flint, having just witnessed Urshifu's raw power, opened his mouth to respond—but found himself speechless.

Urshifu stood proudly in the arena, its fur flowing with confidence, exuding an aura of unwavering dominance.

Johan… you've betrayed the revolution.

"Urshifu's fighting style reminds me of Cynthia's Garchomp," Aaron mused.

Yeah, no kidding. It was one-hit KO after one-hit KO.

"Garrr."

Garchomp let out a low growl, neither confirming nor denying the statement.

Kabu's expression was grim as he sent out his fifth Pokémon—Salazzle!

A sleek, black-scaled lizard Pokémon, standing on two legs, with red markings on its belly and an intoxicating aroma in the air.

Salazzle, the Toxic Lizard Pokémon, Poison/Fire-type! Only female Salandit could evolve into Salazzle, and the pheromones it secreted were highly valued in rare perfumes.

Though Salazzle looked fragile, it held its ground.

But Urshifu didn't care about its opponent's appearance.

"Garruu—"

For the first time since the battle began, Johan took the initiative.

"Surging Strikes!"

"Salazzle, Protect!"

Salazzle immediately created a green protective barrier—trying to block the attack.

However—

Something shocking happened.

Urshifu's water-infused fist pierced right through Protect and slammed into Salazzle.

BAM! BAM! BAM!!

"A one-shot victory!" the announcer stammered. "Urshifu ignored Protect completely!"

Unseen Fist.

Because Urshifu's Ability was Unseen Fist.

A stunned silence swept through the arena.

Kabu and Salazzle had clearly been caught off guard.

Unseen Fist.

[Unseen Fist: If the user's attack makes contact, it bypasses Protect and similar defensive moves, dealing normal damage.]

This was one of the reasons why Urshifu dominated battles in the games.

It completely ignored defensive tactics—a sheer, overwhelming force.

Clearly, both Kabu and Salazzle had underestimated this.

Now, with only one Pokémon left—Ninetales—Kabu's chances of a comeback were slim.

In the end—

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu stood tall in the battlefield, roaring toward the sky.

A complete sweep!!

"Johan wins!!"

"Unbelievable!!"

"Urshifu is unstoppable!!"

The crowd erupted in cheers, celebrating the stunning one-versus-six victory.

Who would have thought?

Kabu, a former Gym Leader and one of the strongest contenders in the tournament, had been completely swept!

It wasn't because Kabu was weak.

In fact, the sheer strength he had shown only made Johan and Urshifu's win even more incredible.

Johan had swept teams before, but this victory had shocked everyone the most.

The stadium was electric with excitement.

Johan and Kabu walked toward the center of the battlefield, shaking hands as Johan smiled.

"Just lucky."

Kabu twitched slightly at that.

"Haha…" He let out a dry chuckle.

Sure, he was the one who had asked Johan to go all out.

But this level of "all-out"…

This was way too painful.

A complete sweep? And he was supposed to believe that was just "luck"?

Still…

Kabu had learned something from this battle.

Watching Johan and Urshifu's unyielding determination, he reflected on his own journey.

He had once lost his Gym Leader title due to arrogance.

But after today—it didn't feel so devastating anymore.

Because just like Urshifu…

He could evolve, too.

He could grow stronger and reclaim what was his.

"I've learned a lot," Kabu said sincerely. "I look forward to our next match."

"The kind where you give me a badge and a TM?"

They exchanged knowing smiles.

# 418: Urshifu · Grandmaster of Tai Chi?

The quarterfinals had concluded, and Johan advanced to the semifinals without any surprises.

However, his method of victory had left many in shock.

A complete sweep!

And his opponent was a former Gym Leader!

Though Kabu had lost his position as a Gym Leader due to certain reasons and had decided to start over from scratch, his strength was widely recognized.

For Johan to sweep such an opponent—who could even take a single point from him?

That said, the competition level in this year's Lily of the Valley Conference was ridiculously high. The remaining contestants all had a shot at the title.

This only continued to raise the tournament's popularity.

But an interesting detail had surfaced among the spectators.

The runner-up of the Indigo Conference—Guderian—had witnessed Johan's full team firsthand.

Because of this, the value of Guderian's runner-up title had skyrocketed online.

Johan, however, only chuckled at the discussions.

He was more concerned with Urshifu's current state.

"Ka-kah~"

Gengar floated beside Urshifu, scratching its head, then hovering up to measure itself against Urshifu's height. It was surprised.

"Hey, we're the same height now!"

Johan glanced sideways at Gengar's levitating feet, which were still dangling in midair.

You sure about that?

"Cha~"

"Goka~"

"Yoji~"

Tinkaton, Charcadet, and Larvitar all tilted their heads up, staring at Urshifu.

Back in the day, the four of them were around the same height…

Now? Urshifu had shot up to two meters.

What were they supposed to do now? Jump up to punch his kneecaps?

"Draaa~"

Dragonite, on the other hand, was very satisfied.

Previously, it had been the tallest Pokémon on Johan's team.

Gengar was big too, but that was just because it was fat.

Now that Urshifu had evolved, Dragonite no longer looked like a towering giant.

"Garruu!! (●｀()●)"

Urshifu crossed its arms, its face serious.

"Enough posing," Johan said, petting his little Ninetales. "Relax already."

"Garruu~ (óò)"

Urshifu grinned sheepishly, scratching the back of its head. Its serious aura collapsed in an instant.

It had been posing for the Rotom phone's camera.

Then, it crouched down and started fighting Larvitar over an energy cube.

Looking at this gluttonous Urshifu, who would have guessed it was the same Pokémon that had just dominated the battlefield with raw power and precision?

But as Johan watched Urshifu devour its meal, his expression gradually stiffened.

A drop of cold sweat rolled down his forehead.

Oh no… oh no!

Urshifu had grown bigger. And according to the "size = appetite" equation (except for Larvitar, of course)…

Its food consumption was going to—

"Garruu~ (ω`)_且"

Urshifu held up its empty bowl, looking at Johan expectantly.

Johan: "…"

Yep. Just as he feared.

Just like when Dragonair evolved into Dragonite…

Financial disaster.

With Larvitar already being a bottomless pit, Urshifu's evolution had added even more pressure to Johan's already tight budget.

After giving Urshifu another bowl of energy cubes, Johan checked its Pokédex entry.

He had glanced at it during the battle, but some details had confused him.

[Urshifu]

[Type: Fighting/Water]

[Ability: Unseen Fist]

[Moves: Leer, Rock Smash, Endure, Focus Energy, Aerial Ace, Scary Face, Headbutt, Brick Break… Aura Sphere, Aqua Jet, Surging Strikes]

[Mood: Excited, Joyful (Who isn't a Legendary Pokémon at this point?!)]

[Status: Excellent (Everything tastes amazing!)]

[Friendship: 100 (Do I look more like Guan Yu, Zhang Fei… or maybe Zhao Yun?!)]

…It's terminal.

But what really intrigued Johan was why Urshifu had evolved into Rapid Strike Style.

Was it because its opponent specialized in Fire-types?

As for why it had evolved, Johan wasn't surprised.

There had been plenty of warning signs.

And Kubfu had already mastered both the Single Strike and Rapid Strike techniques.

Other Kubfu had to climb the Towers of Two Fists and read the scrolls to evolve because they needed to learn one of the two techniques.

But in reality, the requirement for evolution wasn't climbing the towers—it was mastering the techniques within the scrolls.

Johan suddenly thought of something.

"Urshifu, aren't you sleepy today?"

Normally, Urshifu would get drowsy throughout the day, but today… it hadn't taken a single nap.

As soon as Johan spoke, Urshifu yawned, stretched, and immediately fell asleep.

But the moment it did, everyone turned to stare at it.

Because—

Even though Urshifu was snoring, its face looked serious.

It exuded a ferocious aura, then suddenly walked to the side and began shadowboxing.

Each punch sent a sharp gust of wind through the air.

Then—CRACK.

Several meters away, the wall suddenly cracked open from the impact.

"Garruu… zzZ."

As if realizing that it might accidentally destroy the house, Urshifu took a few steps back before resuming its practice.

But every punch still carried a terrifying force.

"Ka-kah~ (ω`)?"

Gengar rubbed its chin, deep in thought.

As a Ghost-type, it normally wouldn't fear punches.

But for some reason… it felt threatened.

Johan, however, understood immediately.

Because Urshifu's Pokédex entry had changed.

Its Fighting/Water typing had shifted to Fighting/Dark!

And its Aqua Jet and Surging Strikes had been replaced by Wicked Blow and Sucker Punch.

Oh, great.

It's a fusion-style Urshifu?!

"Wait a second… did Master Mustard forget to tell me something important?" Johan muttered.

After all, both Urshifu styles had identical base stats and similar fighting techniques—only their typings and move styles differed.

"So… does this mean that Urshifu is more aggressive when asleep because it's in Single Strike Style?"

"And more active when awake because it's in Rapid Strike Style?"

It was like a split personality.

No, a split "bear"sonality.

Then, a crazy idea popped into Johan's head.

What if Urshifu could merge both styles…

Combining the raw power of Single Strike with the fluidity of Rapid Strike?

Would that create a new form?

Urshifu · Grandmaster of Tai Chi?

Johan chuckled.

Merging two different martial arts styles wasn't easy.

Even Mustard hadn't achieved it.

It was just wishful thinking.

"Cha~!"

Tinkaton walked up to Urshifu and tapped its knee.

"Garruu? zzZ"

Urshifu looked confused.

"Cha~ Cha~!"

Give me your hands—I'll make you new knuckle guards!

Tinkaton had recently crafted a set for Kubfu, but now that it had evolved, they no longer fit.

"Garruu~ zzZ"

Urshifu nodded and handed over its now-too-small knuckle guards.

"Yoji~"

Larvitar waddled over, grinning as it pointed at the tiny knuckle guards.

You're not using them anymore… can I have them?

Then—CRUNCH.

Larvitar ate them.

Johan: "…"

"Larvitar… are minerals not enough for you anymore?"

Before he could even react—

"Johan! The semifinal matchups have been announced!"

Johan raised his hand to stop Rotom. "Let me guess…"

"It's either Tobias or Guzma."

"Correct! Your next opponent—

Guzma from Alola!"

Johan smirked.

"Time to crush him tomorrow."

January 22.

Lily of the Valley Conference—Semifinals, begin!

# 419: You Won’t Even Call Me ‘Teacher’

"Johan, don't hold back—crush him!"

Among everyone, Flint was undoubtedly the most excited about Johan facing Guzma.

Early in the morning, he banged on Johan's door, fully displaying the unyielding spirit of a Fire-type Trainer.

"Gengar, put him back to sleep."

Johan, rubbing his temples and yawning, was already covered in exhaustion lines.

Yesterday, helping Urshifu evolve had drained a lot of his Aura energy.

Since his match was scheduled for the afternoon, he had planned to sleep in.

But then this idiot Flint…

"Ka-kah~"

Gengar cackled and suddenly popped up in front of Flint.

Flint's face drained of color. "Wait, no—"

BAM—

He collapsed onto the floor, fast asleep.

Luckily, Gengar knew its limits and only used a standard Hypnosis, not a nightmare.

Then, Gengar casually tapped the Poké Ball on Flint's waist.

With a flash of red light, Infernape emerged, yawning.

It looked at Gengar.

Then at Flint's unconscious body.

And instantly understood.

Without hesitation, Infernape picked up Flint, yawned again, waved at Gengar, and carried him back to his room.

The whole sequence was so smooth and practiced that it was heartbreaking.

After all that noise, Johan was fully awake.

His Gallade and the others had already left for work, so he could only make himself a cup of coffee, grab a chair, and sit in the courtyard reading.

Lately, he had been focused on reading "The Secrets of the Earth", occasionally imparting some knowledge to Larvitar.

After all, among his Pokémon, the only Ground-type move they really used was Dig.

…Well, not exactly.

There was one Pokémon that knew Ground-type moves—

Dragonite.

If Giovanni ever found out that Johan's "Secrets of the Earth" had been studied the most by a Flying-type Pokémon, he might just show up at his door.

In the courtyard, Dragonite and Larvitar—two Pokémon with the biggest height difference—were having a training session.

Dragonite raised its foot, about to stomp down, when it noticed Johan watching.

It chuckled awkwardly and gently set its foot down.

"Draaa~"

That was Earthquake.

"Yoji?"

Larvitar scratched the small horn on its head.

Then, Dragonite raised its foot again and lightly stomped down.

"Draaa~"

That was Stomp.

"Yoji? (O_O)?"

Larvitar's tiny head filled with question marks.

Was there even a difference?

Just pay attention.

Dragonite gently patted Larvitar on the head.

"Yoji…"

Larvitar chewed on its finger, looked at its tiny feet, and wobbled them a little.

Forget it.

It was all just stomping anyway.

Oh, right—

Johan had said to "feel the pulse and breath of the earth."

So, Larvitar mimicked Dragonite, slowly raised its foot… and stomped down.

BOOM!!

A thunderous crash erupted.

Johan's head snapped up.

The other Pokémon all turned to look.

And then—

From Larvitar's foot, a thin crack spread outward.

The farther it went, the larger the fissure became.

It extended all the way to the house—

and collapsed an entire section of the building.

Johan: "???"

No way.

After all the precautions he took against Gengar and the others…

It was Larvitar who got the first demolition?!

Who would have thought that a Pokémon that just hatched days ago could cause such destruction?

At the same time, in Johan's Pokédex, Larvitar's move list updated.

A new move appeared—High Horsepower!

??

Now Johan was completely confused.

He had been watching Dragonite teach Larvitar.

He had even warned Dragonite not to destroy anything.

But Dragonite only knew Earthquake and Stomp.

So how did Larvitar learn High Horsepower?!

"Yoji."

Larvitar flashed a cute smile.

Johan's frustration melted away.

How was he supposed to be mad at that?

At least…

"It's a player village house."

Maybe the Sinnoh League would cover the damage?

Johan snapped out of his thoughts just in time to see Larvitar happily running toward the collapsed house.

"What are you doing?"

"Yoji~!"

Don't waste it, duh!

—

That afternoon, the sky was slightly overcast.

Since he had nowhere to go, Johan spent the morning lounging at Cynthia's place.

According to the news, Tobias had already made it to the finals—

with zero suspense.

His opponent had been lucky just to make it to the semifinals.

Their bragging rights would probably be more valuable than an actual championship.

…Though, of course, not as valuable as Guderian's runner-up title from the Indigo Conference.

Now, the second semifinal match was about to begin.

The massive main stadium was packed.

Even outside, the square was overflowing with people watching the giant screen.

The cheers never stopped—and Johan was by far the crowd favorite.

"Johan's popularity keeps growing…"

Even Lucian couldn't help but comment.

Among their entire group, only Cynthia might be able to compete with that level of fame.

"Only one spot in the finals remains. Who will claim it? Let's find out!"

The host's voice echoed throughout the stadium.

Johan and Guzma walked onto the field.

The two were stark contrasts.

Johan yawned loudly, adjusted his glasses, and looked half-asleep.

Guzma, hands shoved into his pockets, walked with a slouch.

His messy white hair and skull-patterned clothes made him look as delinquent as ever.

When Guzma saw Johan's sleepy look, he frowned.

Was this a provocation?

Johan smiled at every other opponent, but against him, he was constantly yawning?

Did he think Guzma wasn't even a threat?

As soon as they reached their positions, Guzma spoke first.

"So you're Johan, huh?" He gave a disdainful glare.

"Hmm? Mm."

Johan yawned again and mentally cursed Flint one more time.

Their "grudge" was only getting deeper.

Guzma's face darkened.

He scoffed.

"So you're the 'teacher' Kukui keeps praising? I'm going to crush you today."

Guzma's history with Kukui was… complicated.

To put it simply—

It was the classic shonen protagonist vs. edgy rival dynamic.

If you wanted a reference, Naruto and Sasuke wouldn't be too far off.

"Oh, okay." Johan nodded.

Guzma: "…"

His forehead twitched.

Johan yawned again.

Finally, as the referee stepped forward, Johan pushed up his glasses and looked at Guzma.

His tone was calm.

"So… Guzma, right?"

"Yeah?"

"You won't even call me 'Teacher'?"

Now this…

This was personal.

"…?"

Before Guzma could respond, the referee cleared his throat and raised his flag.

"Both trainers, send out your Pokémon!"

Johan tossed his Poké Ball.

"You're up, Dragonite."

A beam of red light shot out.

A chubby orange figure flapped its tiny wings, stretched its neck, and let out a deep roar.

"Draaa~! (ˊˋ)"

It looked harmless.

But everyone knew—this was a pseudo-legendary.

Guzma smirked when he saw Dragonite.

"Going all out from the start, huh? Fine by me."

He grabbed a Poké Ball and tossed it forward.

"Rain Moth, let's put it in its place."

With a flash of red light, Masquerain appeared on the battlefield.

It had two pairs of wings, but what stood out were the large, eye-patterned antennae, giving it the eerie appearance of a floating face.

Masquerain, the Eyeball Pokémon. A Bug/Flying-type that uses the eye patterns on its feelers to intimidate foes.

The longer you stare at it, the creepier it gets.

Intimidate Ability, activated!

This was the only Bug-type Pokémon with the Intimidate Ability.

One of the "Big Three" intimidators in competitive battles.

"Draaa~"

Dragonite flinched slightly, caught off guard by Masquerain's unsettling gaze.

Had the battle already started, it might have had time to counter the effect.

But the pre-battle activation meant it lost one stage of Attack.

This was also why Guzma smirked in satisfaction.

Johan had clearly expected him to lead with Golisopod, the Pokémon he usually used to overwhelm opponents.

Instead, he threw out Masquerain first—a Pokémon he hadn't used in the tournament before.

Johan's pre-match strategy had been disrupted.

And Guzma loved catching opponents off guard.

"Battle, begin!" The referee dropped his flag.

The entire stadium fell silent.

Guzma wore his usual cocky grin and stayed quiet.

He was confident in his ability to read opponents, so he wanted Johan to move first.

Against Lana's Primarina back in Sun & Moon, he had used this same strategy, forcing opponents to attack first while he countered with devastating precision.

But against Johan…

Johan smiled slightly and said, "Dragon Dance."

Guzma's smirk froze.

Crap.

He had just handed Johan a free setup.

"Draaa!"

Dragonite flapped its wings, power surging through its body.

Its Attack and Speed both increased!

Guzma: "…"

He realized something very stupid.

Trying psychological warfare against a trained psychologist was… not the smartest move.

"Masquerain, U-turn!"

From the very first move of the battle, Guzma had already decided to switch out.

This caught the audience off guard.

"Wait, the battle just started, and he's already U-turning?"

Dragonite, despite its round, chubby body, was remarkably agile.

It smoothly dodged Masquerain's attack as the Bug-type retreated into its Poké Ball.

Johan rubbed his face to wake up a little.

That was close.

He nearly fell behind right away.

If Guzma hadn't overthought things, this opening would have been a lot rougher.

Or, as Gengar would put it—

"You're actually kind of nice, huh?"

Guzma: "…"

# 420: Johan—Next!

"Guzma is switching Pokémon—entering the field is Orbeetle!"

At first glance, Orbeetle looked like a seven-spotted ladybug, but beyond its beetle-like shell, it also had a humanoid body, complete with arms, a torso, a waist, and legs.

Orbeetle, the Seven Spot Pokémon. A Bug/Psychic-type known for its incredible intelligence, making it one of the smartest Bug Pokémon.

The moment Orbeetle landed, its eyes glowed faintly with psychic energy—it had just scanned Dragonite's held item—a Sharp Beak!

"Orbeetle, Trick Room!"

Guzma, with both hands still in his pockets, had learned his lesson from before. This time, he went for immediate control.

Buzz—

With the flapping of Orbeetle's wings, a twisted, transparent force field spread outward, reversing the speed order.

But Dragonite remained unfazed.

Its body shimmered white, and it rushed toward Orbeetle at extreme speed.

"That's Extreme Speed!" the announcer shouted. "Priority moves like Extreme Speed are unaffected by Trick Room!"

Plus, Orbeetle was already fast—so Trick Room didn't give it a huge advantage anyway.

BAM!

Orbeetle was knocked backward by Dragonite's charge.

But Guzma didn't panic—he even chuckled slightly and ordered:

"U-turn!"

BZZT!

Orbeetle's thin, translucent wings vibrated rapidly, its body glowing pale blue as it spun around and rammed into Dragonite.

As long as the attack landed, Orbeetle would be recalled to its Poké Ball, allowing Guzma to send out a new Pokémon.

"Did he teach every single one of his Pokémon U-turn?"

"Wait, if every Pokémon uses U-turn once per switch, couldn't he just wear down his opponent little by little?"

"This strategy is kinda dirty… it's got Johan's signature style."

Infinite U-turn switching?

If Stealth Rock was on the field, Guzma's team would be in big trouble.

But Johan had already identified Guzma's game plan—there was no way he'd fall for the same trick twice.

As the saying goes—"The same move won't work twice against a Saint Seiya!"

Johan calmly commanded: "Air Slash."

"Draaa~!"

Dragonite's small wings flapped rapidly, summoning a violent gust that carried two bright white blades straight toward Orbeetle.

BAM! BAM!

A dull impact sounded.

CRACK!

A crack appeared on Orbeetle's shell, and as it let out a pained cry, its body suddenly stiffened—

Flinch!

U-turn was forcibly interrupted!

Huh?

Guzma's expression froze.

Was this a mixed-attacker Dragonite?!

And its luck was this good?!

"Orbeetle flinched!" the announcer's voice boomed excitedly. "It looks like luck is finally on Johan's side!"

"Wing Attack."

Johan's voice sounded again, and Dragonite flapped its wings, striking Orbeetle with another blow.

BAM!!

"Orbeetle is unable to battle!" the referee immediately announced.

BOOM!!

On the giant screen, one of Guzma's Poké Balls turned gray.

Johan secured the first victory, and the crowd erupted in excitement.

"Trying to play dirty against Johan? Useless!"

"I hereby declare Johan the true master of underhanded tactics!"

"He actually got lucky for once—I'm so mad!"

Dragonite hovered in the air, its expression unchanged.

Luck?

That was pure skill.

Just basic mechanics—no need to overreact.

"Nice one!" Flint punched the air in the VIP box. "That punk was getting cocky—make him pay!"

…Flint calling someone else cocky was pure irony.

On the battlefield, Guzma expressionlessly recalled Orbeetle.

Normally, he would scold his Pokémon for losing, but this time, he didn't.

In his mind, Johan had just gotten lucky.

There was nothing to criticize.

Guzma pulled out another Poké Ball, his expression more serious than before.

"Crush it, Vikavolt!"

A buzzing "zzzt" sound filled the air as a large, blue-shelled stag beetle with golden sparks crackling around its pincers appeared on the field.

Vikavolt, the Stag Beetle Pokémon. A Bug/Electric-type.

Clearly, this was a counter specifically prepared for Flying-types.

Vikavolt had extremely high Special Attack, but its biggest weakness was its low Speed.

However—

With Trick Room in effect, everything had changed.

"Vikavolt, Discharge!"

"Kurrraa!!"

Vikavolt's underbelly glowed golden, its massive pincers crackling with electricity.

BOOM!

A terrifying surge of electricity erupted, engulfing the battlefield in a blinding golden light.

But at that moment—

FWOOSH!!

A massive surge of red-hot flames erupted from Dragonite's direction, colliding with the electricity.

The two forces clashed violently, sending shockwaves through the air.

It was Dragonite's Flamethrower!

"The flames successfully neutralized the electric surge!" the announcer called out.

Guzma gritted his teeth.

But then—

As Vikavolt's electric attack faded, a golden, thread-like wave of energy suddenly rushed toward Dragonite.

"By using Discharge as cover, Vikavolt has launched an Electroweb!"

But—

A matching golden thread shot out from Dragonite's side.

Both Electrowebs collided midair and exploded.

BOOM!!

The golden sparks scattered like a spider's web dissolving in the wind.

"Ohhhh! Johan seems to have predicted the Electroweb and countered it with one of his own!"

Using moves to counter moves instead of dodging—this was a classic strategy among veteran Trainers.

Guzma took a deep breath.

He hadn't expected all his strategies to be countered so smoothly.

His eyes narrowed sharply.

There's no other choice—time for a direct confrontation!

At least, with Trick Room still active, I still have the upper hand!

"Thunderbolt!"

Guzma finally raised his hand, slashing it through the air.

BZZT!!

A thick bolt of electricity streaked toward Dragonite.

"Dodge with Extreme Speed!" Johan frowned.

Guzma was right—as long as Trick Room was in effect, he held the advantage.

If Johan wanted Dragonite to avoid taking unnecessary damage, stalling out Trick Room was the best option.

But then—

"Chase it down!"

Buzz—

Vikavolt's wings vibrated rapidly, chasing after its own Thunderbolt!

A sinister grin spread across Guzma's face. "Charge!"

Vikavolt reabsorbed its own Thunderbolt, supercharging itself with energy!

Though this strategy caused it some recoil damage, it also boosted its next Electric-type attack.

Johan's eyes narrowed.

Self-charging… wouldn't that cause a short circuit?

In this overloaded state, Vikavolt was now extremely dangerous—a single Zap Cannon could be devastating.

But Zap Cannon's accuracy was abysmal—even Guzma wasn't reckless enough to rely on it.

Instead, he went for a safer choice.

"Battle is destruction—Discharge again! Flatten the battlefield!"

BZZZZT—

Golden electricity exploded outward, tearing through the battlefield, cracking the ground beneath it.

There was no dodging this one.

Johan sighed, his eyes sharpening.

If we can't dodge, then we'll fight head-on.

"Dragonite, Fire Punch!"

Dragonite flapped its wings, spinning midair before diving straight into the golden storm of electricity.

ZAP!

Sparks crackled against its scales, but over half of the electricity was absorbed by its protective skin.

Multiscale activated—reducing the damage by half.

Relying on its natural durability, Dragonite rushed toward Vikavolt, its chubby fist ablaze.

BAM!!

Vikavolt was sent flying.

At that moment—Trick Room faded.

The battle momentum instantly shifted.

"Chase it down—again." Johan's eyes gleamed.

BAM!!

Despite Guzma's desperate attempt to dodge, Vikavolt was just too slow.

"Vikavolt is unable to battle!"

BOOM!!

Another Poké Ball on the screen dimmed.

Score: 2-0.

Guzma clenched his teeth and recalled Vikavolt.

But without hesitation, he threw out his next Poké Ball.

"Guzma's fourth Pokémon is—Pinsir!"

Johan smirked coldly.

"Next."

# 421: "The Juice Is Alright—A Bit Sour—Don't Buy It Again"

Guzma finally snapped out of his daze from Dragonite's overwhelming power.

He cast a deep look at the raging Dragonite before him, then did absolutely nothing.

"Guzma, please send out your Pokémon," the referee reminded him.

Yet, Guzma stood still, hands in his pockets, making no move to throw a Poké Ball.

The referee frowned, his tone more firm this time. "Guzma, send out your Pokémon now."

At this point, the audience caught on.

This guy...

He's stalling!

He's waiting for Outrage to wear off!

Once Outrage ends, Dragonite will become confused, giving Guzma the perfect opportunity to counterattack.

But—

Blatantly refusing to send out a Pokémon just to run down the clock?

That was a first.

"Johan finally has a real opponent!"

"This strategy is so scummy!"

"Guzma, you're a genius!"

"..."

Even Johan was a little speechless.

Guzma really was a product of his mentor Hala, showing traces of Gold's playstyle—a dirty but effective approach.

As the audience grew restless, Guzma remained unbothered by their complaints.

It wasn't until the referee prepared to issue a penalty that Guzma finally, lazily, tossed a Poké Ball.

"Guzma finally sends out his fifth Pokémon—Golisopod!"

Its massive, armored body landed with a heavy thud, dark claws gleaming coldly under the stadium lights.

Golisopod, the Hard Scale Pokémon. A Bug/Water-type that will use any means necessary to win.

**Wild Golisopod often challenge Grapploct to battles—**but the loser of these fights becomes the winner's meal.

This was Guzma's signature Pokémon.

Golisopod had one of the most intimidating designs among Bug-types, resembling a samurai clad in heavy armor.

However, it suffered from a crippling Ability—Emergency Exit.

[Emergency Exit: If the Pokémon's HP drops below half, it automatically switches out.]

Unlike Slaking and Regigigas, whose Abilities completely ruin them, Golisopod's "Emergency Exit" is at least somewhat usable.

With Guzma's ace finally on the field, the crowd settled down and focused back on the battle.

The match resumed!

Thanks to Guzma's delay tactics, Dragonite's Outrage was weakening.

"Draaa!!"

With a furious roar, Dragonite charged straight at Golisopod.

But Golisopod, instead of retreating, stood its ground and counterattacked.

"First Impression!" Guzma shouted.

[First Impression: A Bug-type move, 90 power, priority +2, but can only be used when entering battle.]

WHAM!

A lightning-fast strike connected before Dragonite could even react.

Dragonite stumbled back slightly.

Guzma smirked and pulled out a Poké Ball.

"Return, Golisopod."

Johan raised an eyebrow.

Oh?

Hit-and-run tactics again?

"Dragonite, grab it!"

Huh?!

The entire crowd froze in confusion.

Dragonite was still under Outrage—how could Johan command it?

And yet—

Dragonite's tail whipped through the air, knocking aside the recall beam aimed at Golisopod.

Before Golisopod could disappear, Dragonite grabbed its arm.

Golisopod: (O_O)?

Then—

With a savage grin, Dragonite swung Golisopod through the air in a massive arc—

AND SLAMMED IT INTO THE GROUND!

BOOM!!

Dust exploded everywhere.

Before the dust could settle, Dragonite lifted Golisopod up again—

AND SLAMMED IT INTO THE GROUND A SECOND TIME!

BOOM!!

Golisopod was completely disoriented.

And then—

Poof!

Dragonite suddenly felt its grip empty.

Golisopod was gone.

"Emergency Exit activated!" the announcer exclaimed. "Golisopod automatically retreated!"

The audience stared in disbelief.

Even Guzma looked dazed.

"Wait... your Outrage...

Does it even follow the normal rules?!"

"YES!" Flint cheered from the VIP box. "Dragonite, smash him just like you smash us!"

He had firsthand experience with this "controllable Outrage."

But with Golisopod forcibly switched out, Dragonite's Outrage finally faded completely.

This was Guzma's moment to strike!

Without wasting a second, Guzma threw out his next Pokémon—

Masquerain!

Intimidate activated!

"Masquerain, Icy Wind!"

Dragonite was 4x weak to Ice, and with Outrage just ending, its defenses were lower than before.

Even if Icy Wind couldn't knock it out, it would reduce Dragonite's Speed, making it easier to finish off.

But just as Masquerain exhaled a cold breath—

BAM!

Dragonite's flaming fist struck first.

BOOM!

Masquerain was blasted away, and before Guzma could react, Dragonite followed up with a massive Flamethrower—

Engulfing Masquerain in flames!

The entire sequence happened too fast.

Guzma was stunned.

Wait.

Outrage ended.

Shouldn't Dragonite be confused?!

Why is it moving like normal?!

This makes no sense!!

"Turns out, scummy tactics don't work against Johan!"

"Guzma stalled for time, only to get completely destroyed instead."

"He brought a knife to a gunfight!"

"Draaa~"

Dragonite exhaled slowly, shaking off some frost on its scales.

Guzma's face twitched.

He was losing control of the battle.

Even though he had spent the entire tournament proving he was just as strong as Johan—

He hadn't even managed to take down a single one of Johan's Pokémon!

Dragonite was just too much.

But as he watched Dragonite shake off the frost, Guzma noticed something.

This was his chance!

"Now! Golisopod, First Impression!"

With a flash of red light, Golisopod reappeared, biting down on a Sitrus Berry as it lunged forward, claws glowing faintly.

Yet—

Just as Golisopod closed in on Dragonite—

A gentle, greenish-white breeze began to swirl around Dragonite.

Its posture relaxed.

Its breathing stabilized.

Its ears perked up.

And within seconds, its condition had visibly improved.

"Dragonite used Roost!"

Guzma's mind went blank.

Wait.

All the damage I worked so hard to deal—

And you just healed it up?

"HAHAHAHAHA!!"

The audience burst into laughter.

"This is just bullying!"

"Dragonite heals more HP than Guzma can damage!"

"Speed? Power? Bulk? Healing? This is what a pseudo-legendary is? No hope!"

Golisopod swung its claw at Dragonite's stomach—but the impact barely scratched its thick scales.

Before Golisopod could escape, Dragonite grabbed it again.

Golisopod's body stiffened.

Its mind replayed the previous brutal slams.

Cold sweat trickled down its face.

But—

Dragonite's attention was elsewhere.

It stared at the Sitrus Berry in Golisopod's mouth.

"Draaa?"

You're eating?

During a battle?

Aren't you afraid of choking?

Here, let me help you.

Before Golisopod could react, Dragonite—

STOLE THE BERRY.

And ate it itself.

CRUNCH.

"Draaa~ (Juicy. A bit sour. Don't buy this again.)"

Golisopod: "..."

Guzma: "..."

Maximum disrespect.

Johan chuckled. "Finish it, Dragonite."

Golisopod panicked, but even its "Emergency Exit" couldn't save it this time.

Masquerain was down.

Golisopod was down.

Guzma's last hope, Yanmega, fell to a final Fire Punch.

Dragonite never fell.

Johan had won.

And the next day—

The headlines of Sinnoh Daily read:

"Johan: 'Guzma? That match just made me sleepy.'"

Guzma's only response was—

"F*."**

# 422: Gengar: I’m Not Gone~

"Johan-sensei, nice battle!"

During dinner, Flint had already lost count of how many times he had gotten this excited.

Johan couldn't even be bothered to respond.

He was already tired—Flint had disturbed his sleep, then during his battle against Guzma, he had to use Aura to counteract Dragonite's Outrage backlash. He had no energy left to argue with Flint.

He'd let it slide for now and just add it to his mental list.

"What are your plans after the finals tomorrow?" Thorne asked.

Plans?

Johan was momentarily taken aback.

After a brief moment of thought, he glanced at Cynthia with a hint of inquiry.

"What do you think about opening a farm?"

"A farm?" Cynthia tilted her head, blinking in confusion.

Everyone at the table was also stunned, especially Thorne.

"A farm?"

Thorne had asked Johan with the intention of discussing the "Special Raid Team" mission.

After all, it was no small matter, and as the vice leader, Thorne was under quite a bit of pressure.

Who would have thought Johan's train of thought would derail so completely and land on a farm?

Johan sighed and shook his head.

"Life isn't easy, the pressure is intense."

Besides…

The café practically ran itself now. The Indeedee siblings, Alcremie, and the occasionally appearing Marshadow handled everything without his supervision.

During the Lily of the Valley Conference, the café's stall had made an astonishing profit in just a few days—comparable to months of regular business.

As for the "Special Raid Team," wasn't Thorne still keeping watch over things as vice leader?

Hunter J and her group weren't making any moves, so tracking them down wasn't easy.

And as for the TV show and screenplay business, Matori and the Team Rocket trio were handling things smoothly. Profits were stable.

There were new scripts, too, but they hadn't found suitable actors yet.

Opening a farm could provide a means of self-sustenance, alleviate stress, and maybe even bring in some extra income.

He already had plenty of experience growing Ice Radishes and Black Radishes.

"A farm, huh…"

Steven spoke up slowly, "Devon happens to have two unused plots of land. If you want, I can help you apply for them."

"If it's a farm, what Pokémon are you planning to raise?" Thorne asked.

Crops aside, raising Pokémon… Knowing Johan, the expenses would skyrocket, right?

"Hmm…" Johan rubbed his chin. "I think Farfetch'd would be a good choice. They're cute, docile, and they come with their own seasoning…"

Do you even like them?

Or are you just craving their meat?!

"As the saying goes, nature's justice is lenient but never excessive."

"Can I work there?" Flint immediately raised his hand, tears of anticipation glistening in his eyes.

Fitzwilliam's ahoge twitched as he caught on. "Count me in too!"

Johan scanned the two of them.

Hire them?

One was a brute, the other a goofball?

"Forget it."

Flint & Fitzwilliam: "…"

For once, you two finally did something right. Thorne secretly sighed in relief.

Johan exhaled softly.

Forget it.

One step at a time.

The journey must be taken step by step, food eaten bite by bite—you can't just swallow a whole Farfetch'd in one go.

Nightfall.

At Cynthia's villa.

"Garrr! |*▼皿▼)"

Garchomp glared at Johan, looking like it would rip him apart if he dared to make a wrong move.

It hadn't missed what Dragonite did during the day with its "Dragon Dance."

But Johan was too tired to care and simply thanked the Spiritomb that brought him tea.

"Thanks, Spiritomb."

He gently stroked its shadowy silhouette.

"Zzzpa~ o(////▽////)q"

Spiritomb blushed slightly and curled up.

"By the way…" Johan lowered his voice and whispered, "Spiritomb, are you interested in becoming the strongest Pokémon on Cynthia's team?"

"Zzz, Zzzpa? d(д)"

I-I… Who do I need to beat?

"Tomorrow is the finals. Shouldn't you be resting?"

Just then, Cynthia, fresh out of the shower with damp hair, stepped out wearing a cute Alcremie-themed pajama set.

"It's fine, I'll crush him with ease." Johan grinned.

Despite his confidence, exhaustion overwhelmed him. He stood up, yawned, and said to Cynthia:

"Good night."

"Good night~"

Wait… what did "good night" imply again?

Lying in bed, Johan didn't have time to think much before hugging Ninetales and drifting off to sleep.

It had been a long day.

The room fell silent.

But soon, a pair of crimson eyes opened on the ceiling.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar tested the waters with a soft call.

After confirming that Johan was fully asleep, it grinned and silently floated down.

It approached the dozing Larvitar, curled up in its "nest," snoring softly. Gengar gently tapped its little head.

"Yoji? ()"

The little one rubbed its eyes, opening a slit, looking confused.

"Ka-kaah?"

At your age, how can you sleep so much?

"Yoji?"

But… isn't there a match tomorrow?

"Ka-kaah!"

You're too small to compete anyway!

"Yoji!"

Oh, right!

Larvitar's little eyes lit up, rolling out of its "bed" and landing on the floor.

It wasn't hurt at all. Patting its belly, it quickly stood up, looking at Gengar eagerly.

"Yoji?"

Are we going out for food?

Larvitar touched its red-armored belly.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar crossed its arms, looking exasperated.

Such a great opportunity, and you're thinking about food?!

It grabbed Larvitar and quietly slipped out of the room, heading toward Cynthia's door.

Before they could even knock, the door swung open, revealing Garchomp sitting cross-armed with a piercing glare.

However, upon seeing it was just Gengar and Larvitar, its expression softened.

Gengar waved at Garchomp, and they all headed to the courtyard together.

Backyard.

"Ka-kaah~"

Garchomp, look at this little one. Exceptional talent, pure potential—it would be a shame not to train it.

Gengar playfully patted Larvitar's head.

"Yoji! (^^)"

Larvitar grinned, waving its little arms.

Garchomp glanced at them before Gengar slid over and handed it a stack of Pokéblocks with a smile.

"Garrr~"

Garchomp swiftly accepted them.

Ahem… Hmm… Indeed, the potential is impressive.

It had heard about Larvitar stomping on Johan's house.

Considering it had only hatched recently, such destructive power certainly lived up to the "Tyrant of the Desert" title.

As a Ground-type itself, Garchomp was confident in Ground-type moves.

"Garrr~"

Show me what moves you've got.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar immediately signaled for a pause.

One house was already destroyed—if another collapsed, where would they sleep?

So it led them away from the competitor village to a secluded spot.

Larvitar then began showcasing its moves.

"Yoji!"

Despite its small size, it executed its moves cleanly—until…

Hummm—

A soft blue light shimmered around Larvitar as it raised its arms, swaying its hips happily…

Dragon Dance!

Oh no!

Gengar's face went pale as it slowly turned toward Garchomp.

Smack!

Garchomp's forehead veins bulged into a cross shape.

It grinned—a terrifying "kind" smile.

"Garrr~ (╬▼皿▼)"

Let's have a talk, Gengar.

Garchomp grabbed the mischievous ghost.

"Yoji…"

Larvitar seemed to sense something was off and covered its eyes.

"Garrr~"

Kids shouldn't watch this.

"Yoji."

Okay…

It peeked through its fingers.

Then…

"Ka-kaah!!"

Flapping wings, startled Pokémon, and silence.

The Next Morning.

Johan woke up, feeling fully recharged.

"Huh?"

"Gengar… did you… get fatter?"

"Ka-kaah~ (._.)"

Today was the final match of the Lily of the Valley Conference, and online discussions had already erupted in full force.

"Darkrai's Dark Void combined with Dream Eater and its Nightmare ability—how do you even counter that?!"

"Lol, do you even know hypnosis? Johan-sensei is the master of sleep tactics!"

"Exactly! Not to mention he has Ursaring—if you've never seen a sleeping Ursaring, you don't know true terror!"

"So you're saying Johan-sensei is basically Darkrai's worst nightmare?"

"True nemesis of Darkrai confirmed!"

"But Tobias has other Pokémon too. You don't really think he only has a Darkrai, do you?"

"What if… Darkrai is actually Tobias's weakest Pokémon?"

"Hiss—Johan-sensei is in danger!"

"..."

Of course, none of these online debates had the slightest impact on Johan.

At this moment, he was engaged in a fierce battle—over breakfast—with his little Pokémon.

"Larvitar! You can't eat the plate! Spit it out!"

Watching the little one gobbling down its meal like it hadn't eaten in days, Johan was starting to get a headache.

Hadn't he fed it plenty before bed last night?

How did one night pass, and it was already acting like it was starving again?

Wait…

Was Larvitar's appetite another one of those "conceptual" problems?

# 423: Lily of the Valley Conference Finals!

January 23rd, Tuesday, clear skies.

The main stadium of the Lily of the Valley Conference was packed to capacity, with tens of thousands of seats filled. The finals were about to begin.

The deafening cheers shook the entire venue as countless voices erupted in excitement.

Under the watchful eyes of the audience, Johan and Tobias entered the battlefield from opposite tunnels, their names being shouted from all directions.

On the giant screen above, their portraits appeared, along with six gleaming Poké Balls for each.

"Johan-sensei, I've been waiting for this match," Tobias said with a smile, flipping his wavy, light-blue hair back.

Johan smiled slightly. "I've been looking forward to it, too."

If he won this match, he'd finally get the tournament prize money. This was way too difficult…

"I have a request," Tobias continued.

"Hmm?"

"If I win," Tobias's expression grew serious, "please have a real card battle with me!"

"No problem."

Johan agreed immediately.

If Tobias won, they'd have the match. But if he lost, then there'd be no need, right?

Hearing Johan's answer, Tobias secretly breathed a sigh of relief. Then, with a dramatic flick of his cape, he pulled out a deck of cards, drawing a confused look from the referee.

Did I walk onto the wrong stage?

Wasn't today supposed to be a Pokémon battle, not a Pokémon card game tournament?

"Ah, my bad. Grabbed the wrong thing."

The referee nearly tripped, but quickly composed himself, raised his flag, and announced in a deep voice:

"Both trainers, send out—your—Pokémon!"

"Go, Tinkaton!"

A beam of red light shot from the Luxury Ball, revealing the hammer-wielding Pokémon. Her twin ponytails swayed as she grinned excitedly.

"Cha! ()"

My big hammer is starving for battle!

Tinkaton had been pestering Johan all morning for a chance to battle, and she had finally been given the opening spot.

It was her first time appearing in the Lily of the Valley Conference.

"Oh no! I bought the wrong tickets! I should have chosen seats further back!"

The spectators in the front row suddenly felt a chill of regret.

You were rushing to buy front-row seats before, but now that you see the hammer-wielding Tinkaton, you're panicking?

At the same time, Tobias threw his first Poké Ball.

"Tobias's first Pokémon is not Darkrai!!" the announcer's surprised voice echoed through the stadium.

Suspended in midair was a massive black-and-blue dragon with three pairs of demonic wings and three heads—one large, two small—roaring in unison.

It was none other than Hydreigon, the pseudo-legendary Pokémon from Unova!

Hydreigon, the Brutal Pokémon, a Dark/Dragon type. It's said to be extremely violent.

Johan had expected that Tobias wouldn't lead with Darkrai as he usually did. He had guessed it would be Latios instead.

But Tobias surprised him. Not only was Darkrai not the lead, but even Latios was skipped in favor of Hydreigon.

This actually worked out perfectly.

After all, Hydreigon, being Dark/Dragon, had a quadruple weakness to Fairy-types.

An unexpected but welcome advantage.

"A major type advantage," Thorne commented calmly.

"But that Hydreigon looks strong," Steven added.

"Huh?" Flint blinked. "Tinkaton is a Fairy-type? I thought she only used Steel-type moves?"

The group gave him a collective glance.

To be fair, it wasn't entirely Flint's fault.

Johan's Tinkaton had always displayed overwhelming strength and mastery of Steel-type techniques, but hadn't really showcased much of her Fairy typing.

The biggest issue was that there were relatively few strong physical Fairy-type moves.

And Johan's Tinkaton was purely a physical attacker—for now, at least.

"Regardless, Fairy-types are immune to Dragon-type moves," Cynthia pointed out.

True enough.

"Battle, begin!"

As the referee's flag dropped, the Lily of the Valley Conference finals officially started!

Tobias frowned. He had anticipated that Johan would send out Charcadet first since rumors online claimed Johan had a habit of using a "weak to strong" lineup.

Now it seemed all those so-called "experts" on the internet were just spouting nonsense.

Still, despite the type disadvantage, he wasn't without options.

What Tobias didn't know was that even Johan himself hadn't planned his lead in advance.

"Hydreigon, use Tri Attack!"

"Garrr!!"

Hydreigon's three heads simultaneously gathered colored energy—fire in the center, electricity on one side, and ice on the other.

Tri Attack was normally a Normal-type move, but because it used Fire, Ice, and Electric energy, it had a chance to inflict Burn, Paralysis, or Freeze.

In the games, only one status effect could be applied. But in real life… If unlucky, all three could stack.

If Tinkaton got burned, paralyzed, and frozen all at once, the battle would be over before it began.

"Cha~!"

Suddenly, Tinkaton's body glowed white as she dashed at Hydreigon with incredible speed, slamming her hand down on its central head.

"That's Tinkaton's Fake Out!" the announcer explained. "It prevents the opponent from moving, causing them to flinch! But it can only be used upon entry!"

The fire gathering in Hydreigon's mouth flickered out.

But before the announcer could revel in his "Pokémon knowledge," the two side heads continued charging their moves, aiming at the now-close-range Tinkaton.

That's not how Tri Attack works!

Sensing danger, Tinkaton immediately jumped back.

"Garrr!!"

The two remaining heads fired off a bolt of lightning and a freezing beam.

The audience held their breath.

Was Tinkaton about to take a major hit right at the start?!

"Cha!!"

Tinkaton raised her hammer, using its massive frame as a shield. The lightning and ice struck the hammer with loud bangs.

For a moment, the hammer was engulfed in ice and electricity, its surface shining with a silver glow.

Meow Meow Hammer—Frozen Form!

"Ice Hammer!" Johan suddenly commanded.

"Cha!! (#`Д´)"

Tinkaton swung the hammer behind her, then sprinted forward, dragging the hammer along the ground, causing icy fissures to form.

By the time she reached Hydreigon again, the hammer had transformed into a massive, ice-coated weapon, and she slammed it down on Hydreigon's just-recovered head.

BOOM!!

Ice shards exploded, sending shockwaves through the arena.

Hydreigon crashed into the ground, the battlefield cratering under the impact. Fragments of rock and ice scattered everywhere as the ground trembled violently.

The audience stared in stunned silence.

Was this a battle…?

Or were they renovating the stadium?

Or were they renovating the stadium while battling?

"Hydreigon, use Flamethrower!" Tobias ordered.

"Garrr!!"

Flames burst from Hydreigon's three mouths—only to be met by Tinkaton's fully-swung hammer.

BOOM!!

The front-row spectators felt a tingling numbness in their seats as pink dust and star-shaped sparks filled the air, blending with rising flames.

Tinkaton, still holding her hammer, dashed away on light feet. She glanced at the soot-stained hammer, then hurriedly blew on it.

Huff—Huff—

A soft breeze passed through the silent arena, revealing Hydreigon lying unconscious amidst the battlefield's wreckage.

"Hydreigon is unable to battle!!" The referee, hovering on a special flying platform, let out a quiet sigh of relief as he announced the result.

Thud!!

One of Tobias's Poké Balls on the giant screen dimmed.

"That Play Rough was devastating—Hydreigon wouldn't have survived even with three HP bars!"

"Not only is Hydreigon knocked out, but the battlefield itself is already trashed!"

"Hydreigon? More like Wrecked-Dragon."

With the battlefield needing repairs, Johan recalled Tinkaton.

"How was that? Fun battle?" Johan chuckled.

"Cha~"

Tinkaton was still worried about her hammer.

The tournament continued.

But one thing was clear—this was going to be a wild fight.

Tobias had already recalled Hydreigon. After a moment of contemplation, he threw out his second Poké Ball.

"Tobias's second Pokémon is—Espeon!"

The audience erupted in murmurs.

Today, they were seeing many of Tobias's hidden Pokémon.

The imagined scenario of him hiding five Legendary or Mythical Pokémon behind Darkrai wasn't happening.

Espeon had a sleek, streamlined body covered in soft lavender fur. Its deep purple eyes carried a mysterious gleam, and the bright red gem on its forehead shimmered. Its forked tail swayed gently, exuding an air of elegance and enigma.

Espeon, the Sun Pokémon. A pure Psychic-type, said to have extremely strong precognitive abilities.

Johan, considering both Tinkaton's condition and Tobias's inevitable strategic adjustments, decided to switch Pokémon as well.

"In that case, it's your turn, Urshifu."

"Garruu! zzZ"

The tall, muscular Urshifu landed lightly on the battlefield, exuding an aura of explosive power. The energy at his feet swirled as his fur gently rustled.

The moment Urshifu appeared, the entire stadium erupted into surprised gasps.

"The sun must have risen from the west! Johan-sensei actually switched Pokémon?! No full sweep this time?"

"In Johan-sensei's words: 'It's the finals, might as well show some respect.'"

"But isn't Fighting-type Urshifu at a type disadvantage against Psychic-type Espeon?"

The crowd was abuzz with debate.

Johan maintained his calm smile.

A full sweep?

That only worked when he already knew his opponent's team composition—essentially when his opponent was playing with an open hand.

But Tobias… Tobias was a different story. His team was a mystery. If Johan underestimated the power of "hidden cards," he'd be in for a nasty surprise.

Besides…

Urshifu versus Espeon… How was that a type disadvantage?

"Garruu!! zzZ"

Urshifu, eyes still closed, radiated an overwhelming presence.

Such an elegant Espeon… Would it cry after taking just one punch?

Gengar suddenly floated in front of the camera and translated:

"Ka-kaah~"

"Coach Bear says: He'll now demonstrate—what it means to split the heavens with a single punch!"

# 424: Tobias – Too Steady, Right?

5 bonus chapter's coming out at 11 AM EST. So I ask that you save you're powerstones until then.

-----------

Espeon vs. Urshifu!

Tobias frowned slightly as he looked at the sleeping Urshifu.

For some reason, today, Urshifu gave him a different, somewhat unsafe feeling.

However, the change in battlefield had already consumed a lot of time, leaving him with little opportunity to think further.

The battle continues!

No matter what, Tobias chose to strike first.

After watching so many of Johan's battle videos, he had drawn some conclusions.

That is—when battling Johan, seizing the first move is crucial.

Although Johan had an extremely high tactical sense and could often counter attacks or even predict moves based on his deep understanding of techniques, it was still necessary to strike first!

At the very least, taking the first move meant having a chance to continue fighting. But if Johan seized the initiative first, then more often than not…

There wouldn't even be a chance to counterattack!

"Espeon, Trick!" Tobias called out.

"Ee-vui~"

Espeon straightened up, lifted its head, and its deep purple eyes shimmered with light. The red gem on its forehead gleamed brilliantly as a wave of psychic energy surged from its body, connecting it with Urshifu.

Trick—a move that swaps the items held by both Pokémon!

Upon seeing this, the audience couldn't help but react with amusement.

"Trick! Never thought I'd see Johan fall victim to this kind of move!"

"Haha—I'm really curious what item Espeon is holding."

"Most likely a Choice Scarf or a Choice Band, right?"

Buzz—

Urshifu's Black Belt disappeared, replaced by Espeon's Choice Scarf!

At the same time—

As Espeon executed Trick, Urshifu moved.

"Urshifu—"

It suddenly took a deep breath, its fur bristling, and its headband flapping wildly in the wind.

Although this was its first time using this move, back when it was still a Kubfu, it had already memorized all the key points of this technique and mastered them completely.

Countless nights, in the silence, it had trained relentlessly.

To an outsider, the practice might have seemed monotonous—just constant punching, over and over.

But this accumulation of effort gave it absolute confidence in this move.

And now, it was finally time to test its years of training!

Johan observed Urshifu's stance and solemnly commanded:

"Wicked Blow!!"

Thorne's eyes widened in shock.

Most people might not understand, but he had studied Urshifu's data carefully and remembered it well.

Urshifu had two different battle styles, determined by the techniques it had mastered.

Based on the moves it had displayed earlier, it was clearly a Rapid Strike Urshifu, which meant its signature move should have been Surging Strikes.

But why was it using Wicked Blow now?!

"Garruu!! zzZ"

The Choice Scarf Espeon had swapped onto Urshifu only made it faster!

In an instant—

A dark aura surged from beneath Urshifu's feet. It clenched its fist tightly, appearing to merely lift its foot slowly—

Boom!!

The battlefield exploded.

In the blink of an eye, Urshifu was in front of Espeon.

"Ee-vui?!"

Espeon's eyes widened in horror. It tried to dodge, but Urshifu's fist was already right in front of it.

And with its ability to foresee the future, Espeon could sense its fate unfolding before it.

Critical hit—explosive damage!

This punch—held twenty years of training!

Boom!!

The stadium shook as Espeon was launched like a cannonball, crashing heavily into the reflective wall of the arena, sending shockwaves rippling through it.

The force of the punch blasted a powerful gust of wind against the protective barriers, causing them to glow fiercely.

For a moment—

The entire stadium fell into absolute silence.

Everyone stared with bated breath, utterly stunned by what had just happened.

After a long pause, the announcer's trembling voice finally broke the silence:

"A—A one-hit KO! Urshifu takes down Espeon with a single punch!!"

ROAR!!

The stadium erupted into thunderous cheers.

"A one-hit KO?! Did I see that right?"

"That was… a Dark-type move? Urshifu's Dark-type attacks are this strong?!"

"Wicked Blow! That's the signature move of a Single Strike Urshifu! I've seen Master Fu's Urshifu use it before!"

"But Johan's Urshifu is supposed to be a Fighting/Water-type Rapid Strike Urshifu, right?!"

"This… is the power of a sleeping beast!"

So this was what Gengar meant by a "Heaven-splitting punch"?

Whoo—

"Garruu—zzZ"

Urshifu let out a faint breath of white mist as it exhaled.

Tobias quickly analyzed the battle in his mind.

The most fatal mistake—

He had misjudged Urshifu's type and moveset, which led to Espeon being defeated before it could accomplish much.

However, Espeon had still played a role.

Tobias took a deep breath, regaining focus.

But we won't surrender so easily!

He pulled back his cloak, eyes filled with determination.

Until the final card is played, no one can decide the outcome of this battle!

"Go, Grimmsnarl!"

A humanoid Pokémon covered in thick, dark green fur emerged.

Grimmsnarl—the Bulk Up Pokémon, a Dark/Fairy-type.

"Grrrrrr!!"

Grimmsnarl let out a piercing cry and instantly conjured a golden, static-charged mass of electricity, launching it at Urshifu at lightning speed.

Prankster!

Tobias's Grimmsnarl had Prankster, which meant its status moves had increased priority.

"Garruu!! zzZ"

Even in its sleep, Urshifu instinctively flared its Aura and Dark energy to defend itself.

"Come back, Urshifu."

Buzz—

Just before the Thunder Wave struck, a red beam recalled Urshifu.

"Why did he recall it?" Flint asked in surprise.

"Urshifu was in peak form, and Wicked Blow was devastating!"

"A Fairy/Dark-type Grimmsnarl quad-resists Dark-type moves, and Urshifu is locked into Wicked Blow due to the Choice Scarf. This was the perfect switch," Thorne explained dismissively.

Most of the audience agreed—it was a brilliant swap.

"Your turn, Ninetales."

Johan sent out his third Pokémon.

A graceful, snow-white figure leaped elegantly into the battlefield, its nine tails flowing like silk ribbons as delicate snowflakes fell around it.

"Oowoo~"

The Ice Ninetales let out a soft, melodious cry, captivating the entire audience.

"So beautiful…!"

"Too stunning!"

The moment Ice Ninetales appeared, Tobias's expression darkened.

One of Johan's strongest Pokémon!

"Misty Terrain!"

"Taunt!" Tobias immediately countered.

However—

Too late!

Ice Ninetales had begun summoning Misty Terrain from the moment it entered the battlefield.

Even with Prankster, Grimmsnarl couldn't interrupt it in time.

Johan had planned this move all along.

Too steady.

With Misty Terrain neutralizing Prankster's effects, Grimmsnarl's ability was half-useless now.

"This is…" Tobias clenched his jaw.

Johan never left an opening.

"Moonblast!"

"Oowoo!"

A radiant, pink energy orb blasted straight at Grimmsnarl.

"That Ice Wall… is that even fair?"

"Ice Ninetales is way too solid!"

"Johan is too steady!"

Tobias sighed.

# 425: Who Said Pseudo-Legendaries Aren’t Gods?!

5 bonus chapter's coming out at 11 AM EST. So I ask that you save you're powerstones until then.

-----------

Boom!!

Amid the roaring crowd, the large screen displayed Tobias's three dimmed Poké Balls.

Losing three Pokémon in a 6v6 full battle was a critical threshold.

Especially when his opponent hadn't lost a single Pokémon yet.

Meanwhile—

At the edge of the battlefield, the tournament staff were scrambling to repair the energy barrier protecting the audience.

It had cracked from the cold—almost shattered completely!

This level of destruction had already exceeded what was expected from competitors in the Lily of the Valley Conference.

"Man, the energy barrier nearly exploded—tell me Johan entered this tournament for a junior division, right?"

"First, he breaks the battlefield, now the barrier? I feel like the tournament staff must be stressed having Johan here."

"As we all know, Johan is notorious for destroying battlefields."

"…"

The audience's chatter didn't affect Tobias, but losing three Pokémon weighed on him.

If he didn't take a point soon, the battle would be completely one-sided.

"…So, I have to send it out now?" Tobias murmured.

"Come back, Ninetales."

On the other side, Johan recalled Ninetales.

If this battle continued, he might actually break the tournament's energy barrier.

And honestly, the Sinnoh League probably wouldn't even make him pay for it…

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales obediently returned to Johan's side. As it left the battlefield, the snowstorm subsided, and the Misty Terrain faded.

Tobias frowned slightly.

The tournament staff had taken extra time to repair the barrier, which worked in his favor but… wasn't exactly fair.

Still, it was out of his control.

After thinking carefully, Tobias made his choice.

At his waist, a Poké Ball shook slightly, as if responding.

"You think so too?" Tobias's frown eased slightly.

"In that case…"

He flicked his cloak, grabbed a red-and-white Poké Ball, and tossed it.

"I'll leave this to you, Latios!"

A sleek, jet-like body descended from the sky. Its blue-and-white form blended with the clouds, leaving behind two white contrail streaks.

"What?! Tobias's fourth Pokémon is the legendary Pokémon—Latios!!"

The announcer's voice echoed the shock of the entire crowd.

The Eon Pokémon—Latios!

A Legendary Pokémon!

The audience gasped.

Tobias had already used Darkrai… and now he was sending out another Legendary Pokémon?!

"No way!" Flint blurted out.

Thorne adjusted his glasses and said seriously, "I never expected Tobias to have a hidden Latios. If he's using it now, Johan must be putting serious pressure on him."

"…This Latios isn't particularly strong," Cynthia said, narrowing her eyes.

Everyone's lips twitched.

Only Cynthia could say something like that with confidence.

"You guys notice he used a standard Poké Ball?" Aaron pointed out.

"Probably means Latios chose to follow him willingly, right?" Steven guessed.

And he wasn't wrong.

The audience exploded.

"Wow, I thought Johan was the only one treating this tournament like a junior division—turns out Tobias is too!"

"Who are these people?!"

"Johan has Urshifu, right? Legendary vs. Legendary! This is getting wild!"

Buzz—

Johan wasn't surprised by Latios's appearance.

As he considered his next move, one of his Poké Balls started shaking.

Dragonite!

"You want to fight?"

Johan smiled slightly. "Alright then."

He lightly tapped the Poké Ball, releasing Dragonite.

A beam of red light shot out—Dragonite appeared, flapping its wings.

"Draaa~!"

Dragonite called out excitedly.

A real one!

It had seen depictions of Latios in murals back in the Dragon Sanctuary. The elder dragons told it that Latios was an incredibly powerful Dragon-type Pokémon.

And now—it was finally seeing one in the flesh!

Of course, it had to battle it!

[Huh?]

Latios tilted its head, looking slightly confused.

Its expression was calm, and it actually felt a bit of affinity toward Dragonite—both were Dragon-types, after all.

But for Tobias's sake…

It had to win this battle.

As Latios's competitive spirit flared up, Dragonite only got more excited.

"Draaa!!"

Let's fight!

The battle begins!

"Latios!"

Tobias felt it too.

Maybe it was Latios's naturally gentle nature, or maybe… he had just realized something important.

A battle doesn't always have to be about winning.

The true joy lies in the battle itself!

"Psychic Shock!"

Buzz—

Latios's wings flared open. A powerful air current surrounded its body as it shot upward, soaring like a jet.

At the same time, a pale blue Psychic energy gathered around it.

Dragonite lifted its head, watching Latios dance freely in the sunlight. Its wings flapped rapidly.

Zzzzt—

Suddenly, a sharp Psychic blade descended from above, cutting through the air.

"Air Slash!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite swung its wings, unleashing two crescent-shaped slashes to intercept the attack.

Boom! Boom!

But then—Johan's expression shifted slightly.

From the collision smoke, Latios's Psychic energy pierced through, continuing toward Dragonite.

Dragonite raised its arms, bracing itself!

Boom!

At the same time—Latios dove through the dust, gathering orbs of white light around its body before launching a crimson energy beam.

Luster Purge!

"Dragonite, Protect!"

A green barrier flickered to life.

The Luster Purge blast forced Dragonite backward, its Protect shield flickering as it absorbed the hit.

But Latios wasn't done.

Its entire body glowed faintly with Psychic energy.

Dragonite gritted its teeth. It clenched its fists—

Ice formed around them.

Ice Punch!

At the same time, Latios rushed forward.

Boom!!

A violent explosion erupted.

Both Pokémon were sent flying, kicking up a massive whirlwind of dust.

As the smoke cleared—

Latios's wing was coated in ice.

Dragonite's arm was scorched black.

An even match?!

Buzz—

The Misty Terrain finally faded.

The next second—

Still midair, Latios opened its mouth—

A powerful Dragon energy wave surged toward Dragonite.

Dragon Pulse!

Dragonite refused to back down.

Its claws glowed bright blue—

Dragon Claw!!

Slash—

Dragon Pulse was cleaved in half!

Dragonite's wings flared.

It charged toward Latios at extreme speed.

Latios didn't retreat—

It exhaled an Icy Wind, covering Dragonite's body in frost.

Boom!!

Another violent collision.

The battle was happening so fast, the audience barely had time to breathe.

From the exchanges, it seemed like Latios had a slight edge—

But neither Pokémon could take the other down quickly.

"Whoa—this is insane!"

"This is the best battle ever!"

"But… will this ever end?"

Then—

A faint white light enveloped Latios.

Its wounds started healing.

Recover!

But Dragonite wasn't falling behind.

As it landed, its own injuries began to fade.

Roost!

"…If they both have healing moves, won't this fight last forever?"

Johan and Tobias exchanged grins.

Then let's see who takes the other down first!

"Latios, Draco Meteor!!"

"Dragonite, Outrage!!"

BOOM—

The crowd lost their minds.

Dragonite's eyes instantly turned blood red, and a violent aura of draconic fury surged around its body. Its entire form tensed up.

"It's Outrage!"

The audience immediately recognized the move. A Dragon-type ultimate move—versus another Dragon-type ultimate move!

But before the crowd could react further—

Johan's voice rang out again.

"Extreme Speed!"

Tobias's expression changed instantly.

Using another move during Outrage?!

Back when Johan battled Guzma, Dragonite had shown signs of being able to resist Outrage's usual drawbacks—but at the time, it hadn't used any other moves.

But this time…

"It's happening! Johan and Dragonite's absurd Outrage strategy is back!" Flint shouted excitedly.

"Draaa!! (◣_◢)"

Dragonite's entire body was bathed in red energy, and it shot forward at extreme speed, charging straight into the rain of flaming meteors.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

One meteor smashed into Dragonite's side—but it plowed straight through it, shattering the burning rock into glowing embers.

Then another—and another—

But Dragonite didn't slow down.

In fact, it only moved faster, tearing through the storm of meteors like a comet cutting through the sky.

Latios's eyes flickered with shock.

This Dragonite—

BOOM!!

Before Latios could react, Dragonite slammed into it at full force.

A deep, resounding shockwave rang through the air.

The entire stadium fell dead silent.

As the dust began to settle—

Latios's body plummeted from the sky, crashing hard into the battlefield below.

And hovering above it—

Dragonite, clenching its fist, gazed downward with an aura of absolute dominance.

"Draaa—!!!"

The moment Dragonite roared, the stadium erupted.

The crowd went completely wild.

"WHO SAID PSEUDO-LEGENDARIES AREN'T GODS?!"

"Screw the term 'pseudo-legendary'—this thing is a damn GOD!"

"A Legendary Pokémon? Who cares? It got WRECKED!"

"How is Dragonite so adorable and yet SO terrifying?!"

"…"

Inside the VIP suite, Cynthia's Garchomp stared at Dragonite, its expression unusually serious.

Johan's Dragonite…

Was a threat.

As Dragonite slowly descended, its Outrage aura faded.

With each steady breath, its usual goofy smile returned.

"Draaa~"

Did I do good, Johan?

The response—

"You did amazing, Dragonite!" Johan grinned.

He was thrilled—but also a little worried.

Dragonite's obsession with battling other Dragon-types…

Rayquaza, Giratina… The world's Dragon Pokémon are on a whole different level.
