# 432: Johan vs. the Elite Four! One Punch!

January 26, Sunset.

Verdanturf Town, Verdanturf Ruins.

"Hmm?"

Officer Jenny, who was patrolling outside the Verdanturf Ruins on her tricycle, suddenly froze. The [Little Growlithe] in the sidecar had unexpectedly leaped off the vehicle.

As a professionally trained [Little Growlithe], it would never jump off a moving vehicle without reason—unless it had sensed something unusual.

"Woof! Woof!!"

The [Little Growlithe] crouched outside the ruins, barking furiously at the entrance, as if it had detected something dangerous.

This made Officer Jenny furrow her brows.

The Verdanturf Ruins had originally been an open site, but after a warning from Johan and Cynthia, the Sinnoh League had temporarily sealed it off and assigned regular patrols.

The [Little Growlithe]'s reaction made Officer Jenny take the situation seriously. She immediately called the Verdanturf Town police department to investigate.

However, after a team of League officers went in and searched the ruins, they found nothing unusual.

The [Little Growlithe] also stopped barking afterward, making Officer Jenny wonder—was it just overfed lately?

January 27, Eterna City.

Boom! Boom! Boom!!

Brilliant fireworks exploded in the sky above Eterna City as hot air balloons floated by, and the entire town buzzed with excitement.

Ding-dong, ding-dong—

From the towering Time-Space Tower, a lively classical melody echoed throughout the city, immersing everyone in the festive atmosphere.

At a grand stadium behind the tower, a massive crowd had gathered—locals and tourists alike had come specifically to watch this exhibition match.

Among them were many of Johan's fans.

Of course, there were also supporters of Cynthia, Bruno, and other invited Trainers.

"Ladies and gentlemen, I am Baron Albert! Now, please enjoy today's exhibition match!!"

Baron Albert, the event's primary sponsor, made his presence known, flaunting his status.

The exhibition match soon began amidst the crowd's excited cheers.

The first to enter was Cynthia.

Her opponent? A rising star from Unova's Pokémon Hollywood—who was also a huge Cynthia fan and had eagerly requested this battle.

As for the result…

"Garchomp, Dragon Rush!"

With one confident wave of Cynthia's hand, Garchomp brutally "educated" its opponent.

A complete and utter KO!

Roar!!

As cheers filled the stadium, Garchomp's menacing grin appeared on the big screen, claws raised in triumph.

For casual spectators, this moment was exhilarating.

For Johan, who could somewhat understand Garchomp's expression… he swallowed nervously but kept a composed facade.

Cynthia's dominating performance set the tone, immediately igniting the audience's enthusiasm.

Interestingly, Baron Albert had paid Flint one million Pokédollars to participate in the event.

That was after relentless persuasion—and with Johan and Cynthia putting in a good word for him.

Flint was so touched he nearly wiped his snot on Johan's suit.

His exact words?

"I knew sticking with Johan and Big Sis Cynthia would get me a solid meal ticket!"

Once the matches began, Baron Albert quickly realized he had made a brilliant investment.

To be fair, while Flint might not be as famous as the other competitors, he was undoubtedly strong.

Outside of facing Cynthia or an overwhelming force, even Johan wasn't certain he could decisively defeat a fully-powered Flint.

"Ladies and gentlemen, please welcome the Elite Four from Kanto—Bruno!!"

The announcer's voice echoed across the stadium.

By this point, the audience was already ecstatic. Learning that a member of the Elite Four was entering the stage only amplified the excitement.

Bruno strode onto the battlefield with a broad smile, his well-defined muscles and battle scars fully on display.

A pair of nunchaku hung around his neck, with two Poké Balls attached at the ends.

In Kanto, many skilled Trainers carried weapons.

Bruno had his nunchaku, Gold had his golf club, Agatha her cane, Erika her bow and arrows, and Red his insulated gloves.

If you didn't have a signature tool, you wouldn't last long in Kanto.

Bruno crossed his arms, eyes burning with battle spirit as he locked onto his opponent.

"Now, let's welcome the champion of the Lily of the Valley Conference—Johan!!"

"Johan!!"

The crowd erupted in cheers once more, their enthusiasm shaking the stadium.

Fresh off his Lily of the Valley victory, Johan was basking in the momentum, drawing even louder cheers than Bruno.

The entire stadium echoed with one name—Johan!

Wearing a sleek black fitted suit and a monocle, Johan stepped onto the battlefield with a bright smile, savoring the audience's energy.

"Damn, Johan looks even better in person than on TV!"

"Why aren't they making Johan and Bruno fight hand-to-hand?!"

Despite his outward grin, Johan was cursing internally.

Why did he have to fight Bruno at the end?!

But he had no say in the event schedule.

And honestly, the organizers knew what they were doing.

Johan had won the Lily of the Valley Conference, meaning he was qualified to challenge Sinnoh's Elite Four and Champion.

He hadn't done so yet, which had left many people disappointed.

But now…

While he wasn't facing Sinnoh's Elite Four, he was about to battle Kanto's Bruno—which was just as hype-worthy!

Johan vs. an Elite Four member!

There was no way this wouldn't attract attention.

And while Baron Albert sponsored the event, the match-up itself clearly wasn't his idea—he wasn't that smart.

Bruno's battle spirit burned even hotter.

"Finally, we meet again, Johan!"

Johan's smile twitched.

Can't I just earn some prize money in peace?!

Fortunately, Bruno hadn't brought his Hitmonlee and Hitmonchan—his strongest Pokémon.

The Pokémon with him had only been in his team for about a year.

Unfortunately, those Pokémon were still Hitmonlee, Hitmonchan, and Hitmontop—Bruno's specialty.

And for a top-tier Trainer like him, one year was more than enough to refine a Pokémon's strength.

But it was an exhibition match—winning didn't matter, momentum did!

Johan unbuttoned his suit, letting the fabric flutter, and smirked.

"Bring it on!"

The "battle energy" instantly soared.

Bruno grinned, removed his nunchaku, and grabbed a Poké Ball.

"Then, it's decided!"

He tossed the ball.

BOOM!

Amidst swirling dust, an enormous figure emerged. The sheer weight of its presence shook the ground.

When the dust cleared, its imposing steel body came into view.

"Garruu!!"

Bruno had sent out—Steelix!

Johan blinked.

A Fighting-type Elite Four member… using a Steel/Ground-type Pokémon?

At the same time, Johan threw his own Poké Ball.

Emerging from the red light was another powerhouse—Urshifu!

Its fur rippled in the wind, its eyes sharp and determined. It spread its arms and let out a mighty roar, standing toe-to-toe with Steelix.

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu grinned.

Not only was it not afraid, but it was itching for a fight.

"Urshifu, Bulk Up!" Johan commanded.

Against an Elite Four member, there was no time for back-and-forth reactions—Bruno could unleash a devastating burst attack at any moment.

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu let out a roar, crossing its arms as its muscles tensed. A dark red aura swirled around its body, its strength rapidly increasing.

Bruno didn't hesitate. His fists clenched tightly as he took a firm stance, shouting:

"Steelix, Bind!"

Rumble—

The metallic grinding of Steelix's body echoed through the stadium as it lunged forward. Its massive form coiled around Urshifu, locking it in an iron grip.

Creak—

The overwhelming force of Steelix's constriction caused a piercing screech, its steel body tightening like an unbreakable vice.

Trapped within the coils, Urshifu found itself pressed from all sides, struggling to breathe.

For a martial artist, this was a grappling technique—a submission hold meant to crush its target.

Even Johan, a non-fighter, could tell that this was a form of strangulation lock.

For most opponents, once they were bound, the match was as good as over.

But Urshifu was no ordinary opponent.

It had a Water-type move—not many, but enough.

"Urshifu, Aqua Jet! Break free!"

"Garruu!"

Urshifu exhaled sharply.

As a master of Soft Style, it didn't try to overpower Steelix but instead focused on using the enemy's force against it.

It took a deep breath, then suddenly compressed its own body, shrinking just enough to create a gap in Steelix's bind.

At that precise moment, a burst of water energy surged around it.

With an elegant, flowing motion, Urshifu slipped out of Steelix's grip, escaping the bind completely.

Bruno's eyes widened.

"Soft Style?"

For the casual spectators, the scene was just an intense struggle.

But to a martial artist like Bruno, it was a display of true mastery.

Yet, despite his admiration, Bruno didn't slow down.

"Steelix, Thunder Fang!"

Crackle—

Steelix's massive form twisted like a coiled spring, using the tension in its own body to lunge forward at blinding speed.

It was like watching a viper strike—only this "viper" was made of solid steel.

Johan instantly responded.

"Urshifu, Surging Strikes!"

No hesitation.

This was an exhibition match—it had to be spectacular.

Nothing was more thrilling than a full-force, blow-for-blow showdown.

"Garruu!"

Urshifu spun on its heel, channeling the momentum of its movement into a devastating punch.

Splash!

A wave of water crashed forward, meeting Steelix's electrified fangs in midair.

BOOM!!

Water and lightning clashed, creating a massive shockwave that shook the entire battlefield.

The force sent Urshifu skidding backward, its feet carving trenches into the dirt.

The audience gasped.

That kind of raw power—and Urshifu blocked it?!

But for Urshifu, this was just the beginning.

"Garruu—"

It grinned, then immediately followed up with a spinning kick.

WHAM!!

The impact was like a whip crack—pure, concentrated power.

But Urshifu didn't stop there.

With a fluid movement, it shifted its stance and delivered another powerful strike.

CRASH!!

The second impact hit even harder, sending a shockwave rippling across the field.

And then—

The final blow.

Urshifu leaned forward, using the full weight of its body, and threw a monstrous right hook.

BOOM!!

Steelix—a Pokémon known for its near-unbreakable defense—was sent reeling.

Even as it dug its tail into the ground, trying to hold its position, the sheer force of the attack left deep grooves in the battlefield.

Pain surged through its body.

Steelix clenched its jaw, eyes bloodshot from the effort of staying upright.

And Bruno…

Bruno smiled.

A deep, satisfied smile.

He crossed his arms, eyes gleaming with a warrior's spirit.

Johan's heart tensed.

A gut feeling told him—something dangerous was coming.

"Urshifu, Detect!" Johan commanded instinctively.

But Bruno's voice boomed over his own.

"Steelix—FURY-POWERED DOUBLE-EDGE!!"

FURY.

An ability that increases a Pokémon's Attack power when hit by consecutive blows.

For every strike in a multi-hit combo, Steelix's Attack had risen.

Urshifu had just landed three hits.

Steelix's Attack had jumped three levels.

And now—

"GARRUUUUUUU!!"

Steelix roared.

Its massive form lifted straight up, its entire body radiating a blinding white aura.

It didn't move like a Steel-type Pokémon anymore.

It moved like a brawler.

Like a warhammer descending from the heavens, Steelix's entire form came crashing down—

Directly toward Urshifu.

The arena trembled.

The crowd held their breath.

Everyone had the same thought—

Even with Detect… Can Urshifu block that?!

The answer came in the next moment.

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu's aura flared violently.

Not just Water energy—but also pure, unyielding fighting spirit.

BOOOOM!!!!

The battlefield EXPLODED.

The entire stadium shook from the impact.

Spectators were rocked in their seats, even with protective barriers in place.

It was as if the earth itself had shattered.

A cloud of thick dust consumed the battlefield, blocking everything from view.

Silence.

Then, as the dust slowly settled…

Everyone saw it.

The battlefield—completely destroyed.

Not a single intact patch of ground remained.

But in the center of the devastation—

Urshifu stood.

It was panting heavily, body covered in wounds—but still standing.

And Steelix?

Steelix was no longer moving.

The stadium went dead silent.

And then—

A thunderous ROAR of cheers erupted.

"HOLY SHIT—"

"URSHIFU BLOCKED IT?!"

"IT WON! IT WON!!!"

Urshifu exhaled sharply.

It had won.

But even as the audience celebrated, Johan's expression was thoughtful.

For just a moment…

When Urshifu threw that last punch…

Had it unintentionally tapped into a different combat style?

Something beyond its usual techniques?

A single-strike, precision-based martial art…?

The style of "One-Hit Assassins"…?

----------

# 433: Mega Gengar—Who Else Wants Some?!

Even though this was just an exhibition match, the audience was completely enthralled, cheering non-stop.

Both sides were elite-level Trainers, showcasing their skill and power with every move.

"Hey, Big Sis," Flint couldn't help but ask in the VIP lounge, "if Johan actually beats Bruno, does that mean he can challenge the Elite Four?"

Cynthia glanced at him and chuckled. "It's just an exhibition match."

If Flint knew that Johan had already been given the opportunity to challenge the Elite Four but turned it down, who knows what his reaction would be?

"I mean, yeah, but… they don't look like they're holding back," Flint smacked his lips.

This was supposed to be an exhibition match?

What kind of exhibition match trashes the entire battlefield right from the start, leaving it completely unsalvageable?

"And don't tell me it's because Larvitar got hungry," Flint added.

Cynthia chuckled, but she had to admit—Johan and Bruno looked like they were taking this fight seriously.

And she wasn't the only one who thought so.

Plenty of people were watching this battle.

"You're back early today?" Clair raised an eyebrow as she saw Lance stride in.

The Team Rocket holiday wasn't over yet, so Lance didn't have to report back to work. Instead, he had been running some simple field missions to keep his skills sharp.

He didn't want to get too comfortable working for Team Rocket and forget that he was still a Top Investigator.

"I mean, it's Bruno vs. Johan. Did it start yet?" Lance shrugged off his cape and dropped onto the couch.

"It's just about to," Clair muttered. "But it's just an exhibition match…"

Lance grinned. "Bruno doesn't do exhibition matches. He takes every fight seriously."

The only difference was whether he was using his strongest Pokémon or not.

But since his opponent was Johan, Lance was sure Bruno would be all in.

"If Bruno's taking this seriously, Johan won't have a choice but to do the same."

This was guaranteed to be an exciting battle.

And sure enough, the clash between Steelix and Urshifu had proven Lance's point.

"Professor, do you think Johan will use it?" Alain stared intently at the screen, afraid to miss even a single moment.

Professor Sycamore took a sip of coffee and shook his head with a smile.

"I don't know… but his opponent is an Elite Four member, so Johan's feeling the pressure."

Back when Johan won the Lily of the Valley Conference, Sycamore had told him that he was ready to reveal Mega Evolution to the world.

Even though there were still many unanswered questions, he knew this power could revolutionize Pokémon battles.

Revealing it sooner would help Trainers adapt and grow stronger.

And besides—if more Trainers mastered Mega Evolution, it would accelerate the research process.

"Professor!" Alain suddenly spoke with determination.

"Hm?"

"If…" Alain took a deep breath. "When Mega Evolution is officially revealed, I want to go on a journey!"

He had dedicated himself to researching Mega Evolution under Sycamore, but now…

Watching Johan battle, seeing the bond between Trainers and Pokémon in action—he realized that he wanted to experience it himself.

He wanted to travel. To train. To fight alongside his Pokémon.

Sycamore blinked in surprise. Then, with a knowing smile, he patted Alain's shoulder.

"Go for it."

They had spent so much time together—Sycamore knew this day would come.

"Take your Charizard with you. See the world."

"Yeah!" Alain nodded firmly, clenching his fist as he stared at the screen.

Maybe… someday, he could battle Johan too.

With so much attention on the match, it was only natural that it was being streamed worldwide.

And when fans saw Johan vs. Bruno, excitement exploded.

"HOLY SHIT, JOHAN VS. BRUNO—THIS IS LEGENDARY."

"Nah, bro, this isn't a battle—it's Johan officially putting the Elite Four in his 'baby food' category."

"Bruno's like: 'Oh, so that's how we're playing it?'"

"LET'S GOOOOOOOOOOO!!!"

Johan, of course, was completely unaware of the internet chaos.

As the tournament staff rushed to set up a new battlefield, he treated Urshifu's wounds.

He had to admit—Bruno hit hard.

Urshifu had a type advantage and still took heavy damage.

Without that advantage? Winning would've been next to impossible.

No doubt about it—Bruno was a monster.

The exhibition match followed a 2v2 format.

Even though Steelix was defeated, Johan had treated Urshifu's wounds, which meant Urshifu couldn't battle again.

So now, the match had turned into a 1v1 showdown.

As soon as the new battlefield was ready, it was time for Round 2.

"Johan."

Bruno was visibly excited. He grabbed another Poké Ball from his nunchaku.

"Get ready!"

With a powerful throw—

"Let's show them what we've got—Machamp!"

The red light faded, revealing a muscle-bound, four-armed powerhouse standing proudly in the arena.

Its eyes were sharp and focused, but deep inside…

There was a raging battle spirit, waiting to be unleashed.

"HOOOOH!!!"

Machamp let out a booming battle cry, spreading its four arms wide.

Its stance was rock solid, its aura rising like a storm.

Bruno and Machamp were dead serious about this fight.

"The Kanto Elite Four's signature Pokémon—Machamp!!"

The announcer hyped up the crowd.

And it worked—the stadium went wild.

"OH SHIT, MACHAMP???"

"Bruno isn't playing around. Johan's in trouble!"

"THE HYPE IS REAL!!"

Even the VIP lounge was shaken.

Flint stared at Machamp's ridiculously thick arms, his jaw nearly hitting the floor.

"Wait… didn't Bruno say he wasn't using his strongest Pokémon?"

He wasn't.

But a Machamp raised for a year by Bruno…

Was practically as strong as a main team Pokémon.

Cynthia's expression turned serious. "That Machamp… is strong."

Even Garchomp had a rare look of respect.

Johan's eyelid twitched.

Big guy, this is just an exhibition match. Why are you going all out?

Fine.

If that's how you want to play…

Johan exhaled slowly and stood up.

"Gengar."

It was time.

Time to test if Gengar was ready to take on the Elite Four.

A serious Johan appeared on the big screen, his sharp eyes reflecting on the stadium monitors.

"HERE WE GO! JOHAN IS GETTING SERIOUS!!"

"OH SHIT, HE'S USING GENGAR! FIGHTING VS. GHOST—THE COUNTERPICK!!"

Johan smirked.

"Go, Gengar."

"Ka-kah!!"

Gengar grinned mischievously, bouncing onto the battlefield.

For a moment…

The entire stadium fell silent.

"A Gengar?" Bruno narrowed his eyes.

His battle instincts screamed that Gengar was dangerous.

Something felt familiar about it.

Then, he realized.

It was the same feeling he got when facing Bertha's Gengar.

This wasn't an ordinary Gengar.

And sure enough—

Johan raised his hand, revealing a shimmering gemstone.

"Mega Evolution."

The entire world stopped.

And then—

The internet broke.

As the radiant glow of Mega Evolution enveloped Gengar, the entire stadium held its breath.

The dazzling energy surged, wrapping around Gengar's body like a cocoon of pure power.

And then—

BOOM!

The glow exploded outward, revealing a new form.

Mega Gengar had arrived.

It hovered above the battlefield, its body now a darker shade of purple, its third eye gleaming ominously.

A chilling grin spread across its face, exuding an overwhelming presence.

The audience was stunned.

Bruno and Machamp, usually fearless warriors, found themselves momentarily frozen.

This was no ordinary evolution—this was something beyond.

"W-What is that?!"

"GENGAR… EVOLVED?!"

"MEGA EVOLUTION???"

The entire League livestream crashed under the weight of incoming viewers.

Flint (watching from the VIP lounge) nearly choked on his drink. "D-Did he just—?!"

Cynthia exhaled slowly, her golden eyes sharpening. "He finally used it."

Even Lance sat up straight, his usual smirk fading. "So, that's Mega Evolution…"

Back in Kalos, Professor Sycamore felt his heart race.

He had theorized about Mega Evolution for years.

He had researched its potential.

But now—he was watching it unfold in real-time.

This wasn't just a theory.

It was reality.

Bruno clenched his fists.

He was a warrior. He respected power.

And right now—

Mega Gengar was power incarnate.

Still, he wouldn't back down.

He smiled, eyes burning with determination.

"Johan… this is incredible," Bruno admitted. "But don't think Machamp will go down without a fight!"

Johan smirked. "I wouldn't have it any other way."

He adjusted his sleeves, his voice steady.

"Gengar, Shadow Ball."

"Ka-kah!"

Gengar lifted its hands, and a massive black orb materialized.

But unlike before…

The air itself seemed to twist around it.

As the dark energy condensed, it became denser—so much so that the entire battlefield felt like it was being sucked into it.

Bruno felt his heartbeat accelerate.

This wasn't an ordinary Shadow Ball.

Johan's Gengar…

Was different.

Bruno wouldn't let it attack first.

"Machamp, Close Combat!"

"HOOOH!!"

Machamp roared, its muscles swelling as it charged forward at full speed.

It threw out a flurry of punches, each strike carrying immense force.

But Gengar didn't move.

It just… grinned.

Bruno suddenly felt uneasy.

His instincts screamed that something was wrong.

Then, he saw it.

A shadow—Gengar's shadow—had stretched unnaturally across the battlefield.

Like a predator waiting for its prey.

"Shit—!"

"Ka-kah!!"

The moment Machamp stepped into range—

The shadows exploded upward.

Dark tendrils wrapped around Machamp's limbs, locking it in place.

It was too late to escape.

Johan's smirk deepened.

"Shadow Tag."

Machamp struggled, but the shadows held tight.

Its body tensed, realizing—

It couldn't dodge.

Bruno gritted his teeth.

He knew what was coming.

Johan's voice was calm and unwavering.

"Gengar—maximum power. Shadow Ball."

"Ka-kah!!"

The enormous black sphere—now charged with unimaginable energy—was launched forward.

It cut through the air like a meteor, aimed directly at Machamp's chest.

The impact—

BOOOOOOM!!!

The entire battlefield exploded.

Shockwaves rippled through the arena, sending debris flying.

Even behind protective barriers, the audience flinched.

For a moment—no one could see anything.

Then, as the smoke slowly cleared…

Machamp was lying unconscious in a crater.

Its four arms trembled, then went still.

The battle…

Was over.

"HOLY SHIT."

The crowd ERUPTED.

Roars of excitement shook the stadium.

People in the stands jumped to their feet, some screaming in disbelief, others cheering uncontrollably.

Johan stood tall, his gaze unwavering.

Mega Gengar hovered beside him, its eerie grin still unchanged.

And then—

It tilted its head.

"Ka-kah~."

As if to say:

"Who's next?"

Ten minutes later—

A scientific paper, authored by Professor Sycamore and Johan, was published:

"Mega Evolution: A Scientific Breakthrough."

The entire Pokémon world shook.

The League's servers crashed—twice.

And history…

Had just been rewritten.

# 434: The Impact of Mega Evolution

Johan, Mega Evolution, and Super Evolution.

These three terms became the hottest topics across the entire Pokémon League network.

Even in reality, the discussions were just as intense.

A Gengar that had already reached its third evolutionary stage had managed to evolve once more!

According to The Authenticity and Feasibility of Mega Evolution, this phenomenon was called Super Evolution, also known as Mega Evolution.

Professor Sycamore's research paper stated that, based on his and Johan's investigations, Gengar was not the only Pokémon capable of Mega Evolution.

The exact number of Pokémon that could Mega Evolve was still uncertain, but some had already been confirmed.

Pokémon such as Gengar, Metagross, Garchomp, and Gyarados had been identified as having Mega Evolution potential.

However, the requirements for Mega Evolution were strict.

Not only did it require the rare Keystone, but it also needed an even rarer specific Mega Stone corresponding to the Pokémon.

More importantly, Mega Evolution required a strong bond between the Trainer and the Pokémon, though the exact threshold for this bond remained unclear.

Even with these limitations, countless Trainers became obsessed with the idea.

The search for Mega Stones was bound to trigger a massive wave of exploration and travel in the coming months.

But that was a story for another time.

For now, Johan had single-handedly shaken the entire Pokémon world.

And it wasn't just about Mega Evolution.

Johan had defeated Elite Four Bruno!

Although he had used Mega Evolution to do so, that power was still part of Johan and Gengar's own strength.

"Mega Evolution is insane! Johan is unbeatable!"

"He actually took down Bruno!"

"Bruno got clapped!"

"Tch, it was just an exhibition match. No big deal. I'd only admit Johan is strong if… he gives me a Mega Stone."

"Bro, I can hear your scheming all the way from Kalos."

In short, Johan had once again blown up in popularity.

This time, however, the impact extended far beyond anything before.

The exhibition match had ended, but the roaring cheers of the crowd still filled the venue.

It had been called an exhibition match, but with the level of intensity, the strategies, and especially the shock factor of Mega Evolution, the battle had felt as real as any official League match.

Bruno's final resistance, Machamp's overwhelming power, and Gengar's ultimate counterattack had left the audience burning with excitement.

This exhibition match had been worth every Pokédollar spent.

And it had also proven one thing—Johan's influence was enormous.

It was obvious that, in the future, many tournament organizers and corporate sponsors would be fighting to invite him to events, offering skyrocketing appearance fees.

Unintentionally, Johan had just advertised himself using the power of Mega Evolution.

Of course, with that influence, more people would also come to him for answers.

There was no doubt that Trainers, researchers, and corporate executives alike would bombard him with questions about Mega Evolution.

Might as well open up shop and start charging consultation fees.

As the waves of cheers continued, Johan and Bruno walked toward the ruined battlefield and shook hands.

"You really lived up to your name, Johan." Bruno smiled.

His battle aura had faded, replaced by a warm and friendly demeanor.

No matter how intense the battle had been, at the end of the day, it was just a battle.

Win or lose, it didn't change anything between them.

Just like how Flint had been beaten by Cynthia over a hundred times yet still called her "Big Sis" without hesitation.

"Just got lucky," Johan said with a grin.

If Gengar hadn't been able to Mega Evolve, the outcome might have been very different.

But one thing was certain.

Gengar now had the strength to go head-to-head with an Elite Four's main Pokémon.

That alone was a huge win for Johan.

Bruno shook his head. Johan was being humble again.

"Regardless, I look forward to a real battle with you next time," Bruno said seriously.

This time, he fully acknowledged Johan as a true rival, someone he would have to face at 120% of his power.

Johan let out an awkward chuckle.

"Holy crap, Johan, you actually took down Bruno!"

Flint nearly tackled Johan in excitement.

After watching such a heated battle, his own fighting spirit was on fire.

And with the Eterna City Festival happening today, the streets were filled with eager Trainers waiting for challenges.

"Pure luck, pure luck."

Johan sidestepped, dodging Flint's flying hug.

"Professor Sycamore has already published the research paper," Cynthia said, pulling out her phone and showing Johan the newly released paper.

"That fast?" Johan raised an eyebrow at Sycamore's efficiency.

Cynthia smiled. "He was probably watching your battle."

After all, countless people had been watching.

"Fair point."

Johan nodded.

He had already told Sycamore that this exhibition match might be a good chance to introduce Mega Evolution to the world in a low-stakes environment.

As a controlled demonstration, rather than a life-or-death battle.

Taking Cynthia's phone, he skimmed through the paper.

He had already read the research data before, but now he looked at the comments and engagement levels.

It was explosive.

Wait.

His name was on the paper?!

Johan froze.

In the author section, his name was listed side by side with Professor Sycamore.

A joint publication?

Wasn't this… way too generous?

Cynthia, however, didn't seem surprised.

To her, this was completely reasonable.

Johan had played a huge role in the research—providing battle data, showcasing Mega Evolution, suggesting research directions, verifying theories, and helping predict other Pokémon that could Mega Evolve.

"At the next Researcher Conference, you and Professor Sycamore are definitely going to be the stars." Cynthia smirked.

Johan chuckled helplessly.

To think that, when he first arrived in this world, he had simply wanted to be a normal Pokémon researcher.

To live as a League employee, enjoy the salary and benefits, raise one or two Pokémon, and peacefully live out his life.

But then, after catching Gengar and Ninetales, he had been forced to battle just to afford their food.

And before he knew it…

He had drifted farther and farther away from his original goal.

"Wait!"

Flint suddenly froze.

He stared at Johan and Cynthia with suspicion.

"So… you both knew about Mega Evolution?"

Johan and Cynthia exchanged a glance.

Then, they both nodded.

Flint hesitated. "What about Volkner and Aaron…?"

Johan patted his shoulder. "Relax. You're not alone."

Flint sighed in relief.

"Oh, good. Good…"

As long as he wasn't the only one left out, he could accept it.

"…Wait, Johan, do you think Infernape can Mega Evolve?"

This question was crucial.

Johan's expression turned slightly strange.

"…Garchomp can Mega Evolve."

"No, I'm asking about Infernape—"

"Oh, Alakazam can Mega Evolve."

"Bro, I'm asking about Infernape—"

"Oh, Gallade can Mega Evolve."

Flint's eye twitched. "Johan. Focus."

Cynthia chuckled softly.

At some point, Johan had developed a habit of messing with Flint.

And honestly, she enjoyed watching it.

For all the times Flint had pestered her for battles, it was nice to see someone else drive him crazy for once.

Johan grinned and deliberately ignored Flint's question a little longer.

But eventually, he sighed.

"…No, Infernape can't Mega Evolve."

Flint froze.

His world shattered.

He turned around and stormed off without another word.

Not asking anymore.

He needed to fight someone to blow off steam.

Johan laughed as he watched Flint disappear into the festival crowd.

He never got tired of messing with him.

Johan laughed as Flint stormed off into the festival crowd.

Cynthia smirked. "Gengar really is a lot like you."

Johan froze for a moment.

Gengar, who had been lazily floating nearby, also perked up.

"How so?" Johan asked.

"Ka-kah?" (How so?)

Cynthia's golden eyes swept over both of them before she tilted her head slightly, lips curling into a knowing smile.

She didn't say anything.

But her expression was clear—You both know exactly how.

Johan remained silent.

Gengar remained silent.

No, no, no. This was just a temporary side effect of the Mega Evolution bond.

Yeah. That had to be it.

They weren't actually alike.

Definitely not.

Gengar thought the same.

"By the way, does Flint have any Pokémon that can Mega Evolve?" Cynthia asked casually.

"Yeah, Houndoom can," Johan said offhandedly.

"You're not going to tell him?"

Johan smirked.

"Not a chance."

He straightened his jacket, adjusting his cuffs.

If Flint ever found out on his own, maybe he'd erase Johan's name from his little revenge list.

But until then?

He wasn't telling him a damn thing.

"Ka-kah~" (Hehehe~)

Gengar started laughing, floating around and clutching its belly.

"...You're laughing way too much, Gengar."

But Gengar kept laughing, even spinning mid-air.

Johan and Mega Gengar's battle had shaken the world.

Reporters flooded the venue, eager for interviews, but Johan refused them all.

For one, it was too much of a hassle.

He could already guess what they wanted to ask—and the answers were all in the research paper anyway.

More importantly, Urshifu and Gengar were still injured.

Even after immediate treatment, he wanted to get them checked properly at the Pokémon Center to prevent any lingering effects.

After confirming with Nurse Joy that they were fully recovered, Johan finally relaxed.

Now, it was time to actually enjoy the festival.

The moment he let them go, his Pokémon scattered in all directions, eager to explore.

The Eterna City Festival had streets full of vendors, making it a paradise for Pokémon looking to eat and play.

Thankfully, the exhibition match paid well, so Johan let them roam freely.

He joined in too.

He picked out festival outfits with Gengar.

He ate cake with Ninetales and Cynthia.

He played shooting games with Tinkaton—until Tinkaton got too excited and smashed a huge hole through the balloon wall.

He went surfing with Dragonite—until they both wiped out and crashed into the water.

He tried goldfish scooping with Urshifu—lost all his money.

He helped break up a "fight" between two Trainers—only to realize they were a couple arguing, and the boyfriend nearly tried to stab him because Johan had almost third-wheeled too hard.

Meanwhile, on the outskirts of the festival, under the shade of a tree, Darkrai suddenly sat up.

Its gaze snapped to the top of the Time-Space Tower.

A strange ripple flowed through the air.

"...Was that a mistake?"

Once was an accident.

Twice…

During Gengar's battle against Machamp, Darkrai had sensed something strange.

Something faint but familiar.

It was Giratina's presence.

At first, it thought it was a fluke.

Gengar's Shadow Ball contained too many mixed energies, making it hard to analyze.

And Darkrai had always felt that Gengar had a small trace of Giratina's aura in the past.

But now, sensing it again so clearly…

This wasn't a mistake.

Darkrai slowly rose into the sky, hovering above the festival.

It overlooked the entire city, scanning everything carefully.

Then, it felt a very faint pulse—something just barely detectable.

Darkrai's body tensed.

It lifted its head, its sight piercing through space itself.

"…This is bad."

Johan's phone buzzed.

He glanced at it, signaling to Cynthia that he had to take the call, and stepped into a quiet alley.

The call connected.

"Johan."

It was Giovanni.

Johan had expected this.

He also knew exactly what Giovanni was about to ask.

So, before Giovanni could say a word, Johan spoke first.

"Yeah, Beedrill can Mega Evolve."

Giovanni went silent.

In his office, Giovanni paused, his hand stopping mid-motion as he stroked Persian's fur.

For the first time in a long while, he didn't know what to say.

After a long pause, he finally responded.

"…Understood. I'll have Matori wire the money to you."

Johan grinned.

Honestly, he enjoyed doing business with Giovanni.

Straightforward. No nonsense. No small talk.

Just results and payment.

"By the way, Giovanni," Johan said, his voice turning serious, "about that person you wanted me to find… I've got a lead."

At the mention of this, Giovanni abruptly stood up.

"Where?"

"Relax," Johan said smoothly. "It's just a lead. I don't have exact coordinates yet. But I believe I'll find them soon."

Giovanni remained silent for a moment before exhaling slowly.

"...I see."

Johan paused deliberately, then added, "Before that, though, I think you should deal with something else first."

Giovanni immediately understood the implication.

Team Rocket.

If he ever wanted a true reunion, Team Rocket could either be an obstacle—or a stepping stone.

Giovanni's voice was calm.

"I understand."

"Alright, well, I gotta go. Something just came up."

Without waiting for a reply, Johan ended the call.

He turned—only to find himself face-to-face with Darkrai.

His instincts flared with warning.

A bad feeling filled his chest.

Darkrai opened its mouth to speak.

Johan turned around immediately.

"Nope."

He bolted.

He grabbed Cynthia's wrist and dragged her away.

"Let's go!"

Cynthia blinked in confusion.

Her face flushed slightly as she glanced down at their interlocked hands, but she still followed his lead.

"What's wrong?" she asked as they ran.

Johan rubbed his temples.

"I knew I should've left right after the match."

If Darkrai was this serious, then the conversation would definitely involve—

Those two guys.

And Johan really, really didn't want to see them.

# 435: You Seriously Cut Down the Time-Space Tower Right From the Start?

"Those two guys? Who?"

Cynthia looked confused. She was curious—who could make Johan so anxious?

Johan slowed his pace as he glanced toward Darkrai, who was silently following them in the shadows of the alleyway. It neither stopped them nor spoke, just quietly trailed behind.

Johan rubbed his temples, looking helpless. "If nothing unexpected happens… it's Dialga and Palkia."

Cynthia's eyes widened.

As a Sinnoh mythology researcher and a native of Celestic Town, she knew exactly what those names meant.

"Are you saying Dialga and Palkia will appear in Eterna City?" Cynthia asked for confirmation.

"Yeah… probably."

"How?"

According to ancient records, Dialga resided in a separate time dimension, and Palkia lived in a space beyond this world.

Theoretically, these two places never overlapped, and Dialga and Palkia should never have reason to interact, let alone appear together.

Johan understood Cynthia's doubts.

But "theoretically" didn't mean much. Otherwise, how would movie plots happen?

Honestly, even he was confused.

Logically, those two shouldn't have clashed this early…

And to be extra cautious, when Johan first arrived in Eterna City, he sought out Tonio to dig for information.

Tonio had assured him everything was normal.

So why did things change so quickly?

The only one who could answer that question now was…

Darkrai, who was still floating in the alleyway, watching silently.

Johan and Cynthia exchanged glances.

They stopped walking and turned toward Darkrai.

"Let me ask something first."

Before Darkrai could speak, Johan raised a hand, stopping it.

"Can we still leave Eterna City?"

Darkrai stared at Johan without responding immediately.

It just… kept staring.

Seconds passed.

Then—

A strange pulse rippled through the sky above Eterna City.

Because of the festival fireworks, combined with whatever power was at play, the city's residents didn't notice a thing.

Darkrai finally spoke.

"You cannot leave."

Johan fell silent.

I strongly suspect you stalled on purpose, and I have evidence!

He took a deep breath, calming himself down.

"When did you find out?" Johan asked.

"About… an hour ago," Darkrai admitted.

Johan blinked.

An hour ago?

In the movie's timeline, Darkrai sensed the danger early, trying to warn the city by scaring the residents away.

But ultimately, it failed and was blamed as the cause of the disaster, leading to a massive Trainer hunt against it.

That took at least a full day—maybe even longer.

"This is happening way too fast."

Darkrai nodded.

It came so fast that there was no time to warn people or drive them away.

That's why Darkrai hadn't even bothered trying.

"How did you find out?" Cynthia asked.

Darkrai glanced at her, then hesitated for a moment before waving its arm.

Something small was tossed into the air.

A floating black letter "P" hovered in place, fidgeting anxiously.

Johan and Cynthia both froze in surprise.

"…Unown?"

The little P-shaped Unown immediately dove behind Johan, peeking out cautiously at Darkrai.

Its reaction was so familiar—as if they were longtime friends.

Johan felt a headache coming on.

Darkrai's expression said it all—"I knew it."

"You're from the Eterna Ruins, aren't you?" Cynthia suddenly realized.

The Unown nodded rapidly.

The Eterna Ruins…

Johan instantly recalled the "prophecy" the Unown had given them when he and Cynthia explored the ruins.

Back then, the Unown warned of an impending disaster.

At the time, Johan wasn't completely sure who the prophecy referred to.

But now…

It was obviously Arceus.

After all, Sinnoh's three movie plots were all linked—everything started because Arceus was about to awaken.

"Let me get this straight." Johan grabbed the Unown-P.

Arceus is about to wake up, and its rage equals destruction.

That rage has disrupted time and space, causing Dialga and Palkia to overlap dimensions.

Each of them thinks the other is invading their territory, so they start fighting.

The Unown sensed this fight, entered the dimensional rift, and tried to witness or stop it.

They failed, so they came to ask for help.

And the first person they thought of was…

The "good guy" who gave them Pokéblocks—Johan.

Then, intentionally or not, they led Dialga and Palkia straight to Eterna City.

That's why they arrived so quickly.

So, in conclusion—

This is all Flint's fault.

If Johan hadn't gone to save him, he wouldn't have met the Unown.

If he hadn't met the Unown, he wouldn't have heard the prophecy.

If he hadn't heard the prophecy, he wouldn't be in this mess now.

This wasn't just one mark in the revenge notebook—at least ten.

Johan sighed deeply.

"So… what do we do next?" Johan looked at Darkrai.

Darkrai looked back at him.

Then Cynthia.

Then back to Johan.

Johan narrowed his eyes.

"Why are you all looking at me?"

"I mean, you're the one who has to figure it out," Cynthia said seriously.

"If Dialga and Palkia really start fighting here, Eterna City won't just be destroyed—it could cause a chain reaction across all of Sinnoh."

Johan forced a smile.

What gave you the impression that I could solve this?!

Johan took a deep breath, calming down.

Wait.

How did the movie resolve this?

He lifted his head, looking toward the center of Eterna City—

The Time-Space Tower.

That's right!

"Oración!"

"Oración?" Cynthia looked confused.

Darkrai, however, reacted immediately.

Oración was the song that Alicia had played for it and the Pokémon in the garden.

It had the power to calm emotions.

"But… Oración alone won't reach them."

Darkrai suddenly turned sharply, looking toward the top of the Time-Space Tower.

"The Time-Space Tower?"

Though it was phrased as a question, its tone was already certain.

"Exactly!"

Johan nodded firmly.

"Find Tonio and Alice. Godey should have kept a recording of Oración. Once you find it, take it to the top of the Time-Space Tower and prepare to play it the moment Dialga and Palkia arrive."

"Understood!"

Darkrai nodded.

Then it asked, "What about you two?"

Johan narrowed his eyes.

"I'm going to set up a backup plan."

Darkrai studied him for a moment, then sank into the shadows and disappeared.

"Cynthia, go get Flint and Bruno," Johan said next.

"If this turns into an actual battle, we're going to need them."

Bruno was an absolute powerhouse.

As for Flint…

Well.

If Dialga and Palkia showed up, they could just throw him at them and see what happened.

"Got it!" Cynthia turned to leave.

Then she paused.

She glanced down at her hand.

Johan was still holding it.

"Ah—sorry," Johan quickly let go.

He had been so tense he hadn't noticed.

Cynthia's cheeks flushed slightly, but there was no time to dwell on it.

"Be careful."

"Meet at the Pokémon Center," Johan said.

"Understood!"

Sinnoh League Headquarters.

"A strange energy field has appeared outside Eterna City!"

Bertha had just sat down in her office when she received the report from the monitoring department. She sighed, rubbing her temples.

Can I not get a single day of peace?

At this point, she was seriously questioning whether being an Elite Four member was even worth it.

Despite her complaints, she pressed the intercom button.

"Where?"

"Eterna City!"

At the same time, a live feed from outside the city appeared on the large screen in her office.

A thick, dense white fog had completely shrouded Eterna City and the surrounding areas.

"All testing confirms that this energy field cannot be dispersed. Drones, monitoring personnel, and even Pokémon are unable to enter. Communication signals have also been completely cut off from within."

Bertha frowned.

This was no ordinary incident.

Eterna City had thousands of residents, and with the festival happening, there were even more tourists than usual.

If something went wrong…

"Any idea what's causing this?" Bertha asked grimly.

"The source is currently unknown, but based on our analysis… this energy field is most likely related to spatial distortions—similar to a dimensional wall! We're already consulting experts—"

The report was interrupted by an incoming call.

Bertha glanced at the screen.

"Professor Carolina?"

She was surprised but quickly answered.

Professor Carolina's expression was grave.

"Bertha, this energy field outside Eterna City… it's likely connected to Dialga and Palkia."

Bertha's pupils shrank.

On the screen behind Professor Carolina, a glowing orb was floating.

The Adamant Orb—Dialga's sacred relic.

It had never glowed before.

This was the first time in history.

And, coincidentally, it started glowing at the exact moment the strange energy field appeared around Eterna City.

Dialga and Palkia…

Bertha suddenly felt a massive headache coming on.

How did this suddenly involve them?

Somewhere in the depths of a temporal-spatial distortion…

Time and space collided violently, their energies constantly clashing and reforming.

At the very center of this chaotic rift, two colossal figures stood locked in battle.

Every time they struck, space would shatter and time would fracture.

BOOM!

The shockwaves from their battle rippled outward, warping reality itself.

Around them, countless Unown floated desperately, trying to contain the destruction.

But no matter how many Unown arrived, they couldn't stop the two deities.

BOOM!

Time froze.

Space tore apart.

Dialga roared.

"Garrrr!!!"

Palkia responded immediately, slashing forward with a spatial blade that could cut through existence itself.

BOOM!

Another devastating collision erupted.

The force of their battle wasn't random.

Whether intentionally or not, the two legendary Pokémon were slowly drifting in one direction.

Wherever they passed, the fabric of reality was left in ruins.

The Unown trembled in terror.

"Cha?"

Tinkaton tilted its head, looking confused.

Across from it, Johan held three ancient artifacts in his hands—

Uxie's Claw, Mesprit's Feather, and Azelf's Fang.

"Yeah… these are the ones."

Tinkaton scratched its head.

Okay, but how the hell were they supposed to forge a red chain out of…

A tooth, a tiny feather, and a small claw?

And why does it have to be red?!

"Ka-kah~!"

Gengar, now wearing a pair of fake scholar glasses, rubbed its chin.

"My expert suggestion? Let's melt them down with my poison first."

"Goka! Goka!"

Charcadet frantically shook its head.

No, no, no!

They need to be red! They should try smelting them with fire first!

"Garrr?"

Dragonite picked up Mesprit's Feather, squeezing it in its hand.

It didn't bend at all.

"Hmm?"

Urshifu grabbed Azelf's Fang, clenched its fist, and squeezed hard.

It didn't even crack.

Unbreakable.

"Yoji~"

Larvitar casually tossed Uxie's Claw into its mouth, biting down.

Johan: "...Are you serious?"

He sighed, rubbing his forehead.

He should've known.

None of his Pokémon were normal.

But seriously, how the hell were they supposed to forge this thing?

Then—

Johan suddenly had an idea.

He turned toward Ninetales.

At the end of one of its nine flowing tails, the little P-shaped Unown was happily bouncing up and down.

Johan narrowed his eyes, his lips curling into a meaningful smile.

This little guy might know something.

The Unown's excitement froze instantly.

Its movements became stiff.

It slowly turned its head—

Only to see Johan grinning at it.

And around him…

Gengar, adjusting its glasses with a sinister gleam in its eyes.

Tinkaton, spinning its massive hammer, its smile unsettlingly wide.

Dragonite, lazily patting its huge belly, looking ready to crush something.

Urshifu, cracking its knuckles, exuding pure intimidation.

Charcadet, brushing the flames on its head, heat intensifying.

Larvitar, using Uxie's Claw as a damn toothpick.

The Unown gulped.

…Why do I suddenly feel like Dialga and Palkia are the lesser evil?

Some Trainers had noticed the anomaly and were trying to escape the city.

But no matter how hard they tried, the thick fog refused to let them leave.

Even if they ran straight ahead, they would always end up back at the bridge.

"We can't get out!"

"What's going on?!"

"My phone has no signal! We can't contact anyone outside!"

Panic started to spread.

And panic… was contagious.

Suddenly, someone shouted—

"Baron Alberto has found the cause! It's a Darkrai! If we get rid of that Darkrai, we can escape!"

Darkrai.

The "nightmare Pokémon."

So… this was all its doing?

The moment they had a target for their fear, reason went out the window.

"Darkrai!"

"Let's go! It's in the town square!"

"Take it down together!"

A large group of Trainers began gathering in the square, preparing for battle.

"Johan, what the hell is going on?"

Flint's loud voice echoed through the center as he barged in.

Behind him were Cynthia and Bruno.

Bruno was carrying a ridiculous amount of steamed buns, stuffing them into his mouth like a starving man.

"A huge fog just appeared and trapped the city?" Flint asked.

"Yeah."

Johan smiled.

"Nothing major."

Flint relaxed.

"Oh, okay, that's fine—"

"—It's just Dialga."

Flint's eye twitched.

"…Excuse me?"

"And Palkia."

Flint: "?????"

"They're about to start a fight."

Flint's expression went blank.

Bruno stopped chewing.

Cynthia's face darkened.

"In Eterna City?" Bruno asked.

"Yep. Didn't Cynthia tell you?"

"There was no time," Cynthia said, looking serious.

"Relax." Johan patted Flint's shoulder with a big grin.

"You'll be leading the charge."

Flint: "…WHAT?!"

"You're in charge of blocking Dialga and Palkia."

Flint: "???????????"

At that moment, a large crowd of people rushed into the Pokémon Center.

Some were carrying unconscious Pokémon, while others were dragging unconscious Trainers who had collapsed.

The Pokémon Center was instantly thrown into chaos.

"What the hell is going on?" Flint asked, frowning.

Had Dialga and Palkia already started fighting?

Johan grabbed a passing Trainer and asked, "What happened?"

The Trainer recognized Johan immediately. "Johan! It's Baron Alberto! He led a group of Trainers to attack Darkrai in the town square!"

Johan's face darkened instantly.

Of course. Of course it's that idiot.

"Move!"

Without hesitation, Johan rushed toward the town square.

If Darkrai had really fought back, things might already be out of control.

As they arrived at the square, the scene before them was exactly as expected.

Alberto—who had now been transformed into a giant Lickilicky—was standing at the front, commanding the Trainers.

Meanwhile, dozens of Pokémon and Trainers lay unconscious on the ground, their bodies completely still.

Johan didn't even need to ask.

Darkrai had put them all to sleep using Dark Void.

"Ka-kah."

Gengar, floating out of Johan's shadow, grinned as it observed the battlefield.

"So this is what mass sleep looks like?"

BOOM!

Suddenly—

A massive crack appeared in the sky above the Time-Space Tower.

A blazing red slash tore through the air—

And sliced off the top half of one of the tower's spires.

Johan's eyes widened in disbelief.

WHAT THE F—

In the original movie, Dialga and Palkia had fought for half the movie before they barely damaged the tower.

But now?!

They hadn't even appeared yet, and the tower was already missing half its structure?!

# 436: Johan—Initiate Plan B!

The two towering structures that defined Alamos Town were the Time-Space Towers—the left one representing Time and the right one representing Space.

But just now, a single blazing slash had sheared off half the spire of the left tower.

Above the towering structure, strange ripples distorted the air, as if time and space themselves were warping—

And within the disturbance—

Faint, distant roars echoed.

"What… what is that?"

The entire town square fell into a stunned silence, every gaze locked onto the mutilated Time-Space Tower.

Then—

Darkrai shot upward, abandoning the Trainers and Pokémon it had just put to sleep.

As it rose, a violent shockwave erupted from its body, kicking up a storm of dust and debris.

Its cold, piercing gaze locked onto the swirling distortion in the sky.

And then, it roared:

"YOU ARE NOT WELCOME HERE! GET OUT!!"

With those words, Darkrai thrust its arms forward—a black sphere forming between its hands, rapidly fracturing into countless smaller orbs that shot toward the distortion.

ROOOOAAAAARRR!!

A thunderous howl rumbled through the air—

And in the next instant—

A crimson arc of energy erupted from the distortion.

Spacial Rend!

It tore through the sky at blinding speed—

CRASH!!

The attack didn't directly target anyone, but it ripped through the plaza, slashing across the town and annihilating everything in its path.

The impact left behind a gaping chasm—

And anything caught in the attack had simply vanished into nothingness.

A deathly chill spread through the crowd.

That attack…

Could they even survive something like that?

If that attack had been aimed directly at them—

Would they even exist anymore?

A ripple of pure terror swept through the town square.

"I SAID GET OUT!!" Darkrai roared again, its energy surging.

And in response—

A radiant blue beam erupted from the distortion.

"Darkrai!" Johan shouted immediately. "STOP TALKING!!"

If Darkrai kept provoking them, this entire town would be gone before they even arrived!

Darkrai hesitated, glancing at Johan—

Then at the swirling tear in space.

Finally, it floated downward.

Only then did the terrified crowd notice Johan and the others.

"Johan!! Johan, save us!!"

"Johan! Oh, thank Arceus you're here!!"

Suddenly, everyone surged toward him, pleading, sobbing, clinging to whatever hope they could find.

Johan rubbed his temples.

Great. Just great.

Originally, Dialga and Palkia would have needed time to destroy the town.

Now?

One Roar of Time. One Spacial Rend.

BOOM. Everyone's dead.

But…

Even though he knew that…

He couldn't ignore their pleas.

"That's Dialga and Palkia?"

Cynthia's gaze lifted, narrowing her golden eyes.

Within the swirling dimensional fog, amidst the flashes of crackling energy, two immense figures loomed in the shadows, clashing violently.

And with each collision, their forms became clearer, the shockwaves more intense.

Dialga.

Palkia.

The crowd froze in place, their expressions going blank.

In Sinnoh, every person had heard the legends of these two Pokémon.

And now—

They were real.

And they were furious.

A creeping, inescapable sense of doom settled over the town.

But just as panic was about to boil over—

Someone spoke.

"Johan is here."

"Cynthia is here."

"Flint is here."

"We have the Elite Four."

Slowly, the fear subsided, replaced by something stronger—

Hope.

"They're coming." Johan nodded grimly.

Then, he turned to Flint—

And gripped his shoulder.

"Your time to shine."

Flint's face instantly fell.

"…Johan." He forced a laugh. "You're joking, right?"

Johan stared at him.

Flint: "Hahaha… ha… ha…"

"…Ha."

"HAHAHAHA!!"

Nearby, Bruno cracked his knuckles, his muscles tightening as he rotated his shoulders, producing a series of loud pops.

"Don't know if I can hold them back…" He grinned.

But—

That wasn't going to stop him.

Because as an Elite Four member, he was Sinnoh's shield.

This wasn't just about being strong—

This was his duty.

And even if he was outmatched, he had no choice but to fight.

Bruno turned to Johan, smirking.

"Johan. You coming?"

Johan's expression twitched.

"…Next time. Definitely."

"Alright. What's the plan?" Darkrai landed beside Johan.

Now, the entire town turned to him.

Waiting.

Johan glanced at the half-destroyed Time-Space Tower.

This wasn't how it was supposed to go.

According to the movie, they were supposed to lure Dialga and Palkia to the tower, play Oración, and calm them down.

But now?

They might not even have a tower left.

Johan's eyes drifted to Tonio and Alice—

Both clutching the Oración music disk.

His voice was firm.

"Tonio. Give me the Oración disk."

The scientist hesitated—

Then handed it over.

Johan turned to Darkrai.

"Go find Tonio's grandfather's lab. There should be a backup copy of the song. Get it to the tower."

Darkrai nodded.

Bruno, meanwhile, was already walking straight toward Dialga and Palkia.

Johan took a deep breath.

This was bad.

Very bad.

But there was still a way out.

He took off his coat and tossed it to Gengar.

And then—

He grinned.

"Alright. Plan B."

# 437: Johan: Mere Twin Dragons!

"How's it going?"

As Dragonite and Tinkaton landed, Johan immediately asked.

Whoosh—

The Unown letter P, with tears in its eyes, flew toward Cynthia and hid behind her.

"Cha~"

"Draaaa~"

Tinkaton and Dragonite quickly explained the situation in a simple and concise manner.

Thanks to their "help," Unown P finally recalled some details about the forging of the "Red Chain," but there weren't enough materials!

According to Unown P, the claws of Azelf, Mesprit, and Uxie were crucial components, but the "Red Chain" still needed a medium—preferably a meteorite.

In the anime, Cyrus indeed used a meteorite to craft the Red Chain after capturing the lake guardians.

But in this situation… where could they find a meteorite?

They'd have to try using ores instead!

"Gengar!" Johan called out.

"Ka-kaah~"

Understanding immediately, Gengar reached into its mouth and pulled out the ores it had previously bought for the Larvitar back in Oreburgh City.

Tinkaton didn't hesitate either, immediately pulling in Charcadet and Rotom to start forging in the plaza.

"What… are they making?" Darkrai couldn't help but ask.

"Something to restrain those two," Johan said in a deep voice.

Darkrai opened its mouth, wanting to ask what exactly could possibly restrain the Twin Dragons, but seeing Johan's determined expression, it held back.

Truthfully, Johan wasn't entirely sure Tinkaton could successfully forge it, but as a Trainer, he believed in Tinkaton!

"So next… it's up to us to face those two!"

Johan flipped onto Dragonite's back, quickly equipping it with portable armor before tossing out a Poké Ball.

"Ninetales, Kubfu, I'm leaving things here to you!"

Then he turned to Gengar with a grin.

"Gengar, let's give those two a little surprise."

"Ka-kaah! (▼皿▼)"

Gengar let out a sinister laugh.

"I'm coming too."

At that moment, Cynthia summoned Garchomp and swiftly leaped onto its back, locking eyes with Johan with unwavering determination.

"What are you doing? Stay here and protect—"

"No, I'm going with you!" Cynthia insisted firmly, summoning the rest of her Pokémon to help protect Tinkaton as it worked.

Johan's expression hardened.

"You need to understand how important this place is. Tinkaton and the others are the key to Plan B—"

Before he could finish,

"You two are unbelievable!"

Darkrai, looking completely exasperated, roared and cut them off.

"If you keep bickering, Twinleaf Town will be gone before you're done!"

As its voice fell, a surge of energy erupted around Darkrai, creating a powerful shockwave as it charged toward Dialga and Palkia.

In the end, if they wanted to protect Twinleaf Town, they had no choice but to fight!

Johan and Cynthia exchanged glances.

In the end, Johan couldn't convince her, so he simply said, "Be careful."

"Mm!"

With a pat, Dragonite took off.

"Draaa~!!"

Dragonite let out a mighty roar, its wings slicing through the air as it eagerly chased after the battle.

"Garrr—!!"

Not to be outdone, Garchomp also rocketed skyward, leaving two trails of vapor in its wake.

"What about… us?"

Flint, struggling to his feet, mumbled as he watched Johan and Cynthia's departing figures.

"Ku!!"

His Infernape smacked the back of his head, nearly making him stumble.

What are you standing around for? Get moving!

Infernape's eyes burned with battle fervor—this was the perfect opportunity to take down two legendary Pokémon!

"Oh, right, right!"

Flint snapped out of it, throwing out a Poké Ball to summon his Rapidash.

"Charge!!"

"Darkrai!"

With the wind howling past his ears, Johan gripped onto Dragonite's armor tightly and shouted, "Forget the fancy tricks—use Dark Void!!"

"You're telling me what to do?"

Darkrai shot him an irritated glare before roaring,

"This is not your place—get out of here!!"

With that, Darkrai raised both hands, forming a massive Dark Void, ready to launch another assault on the Twin Dragons.

"Don't be reckless! One against two? Focus on Dialga!!" Johan's voice rang out again.

Darkrai froze.

Why?

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar appeared beside Darkrai, pointing at Palkia's left shoulder.

The crimson jewel embedded in Palkia's shoulder, which was supposed to shine brilliantly, was now covered in cracks.

Clearly, Palkia had been too confident in its strength, taking a direct hit from Dialga's Roar of Time during their earlier clash, leaving it injured.

So throughout their battle, Dialga had been suppressing Palkia.

In this situation, they had to intervene!

It would buy them more time.

Darkrai understood immediately.

That makes sense!

"You're dumber than my Gengar," Johan scoffed.

Darkrai shot him another glare.

But it didn't argue back. Instead, it hurled the massive Dark Void directly at Dialga.

"Garrr!!"

Seeing this, Dialga immediately enveloped itself in a temporal barrier to resist the attack, then glared furiously at Darkrai.

Why just me?!

Darkrai, now completely fed up with Johan but absolutely fearless against Dialga and Palkia, roared back:

"What are you staring at?! I'm hitting YOU!"

With that, it lunged straight for Dialga.

Palkia hesitated for a moment, then seemed to realize—was this thing on its side?

Dialga was enraged.

Now it was a two-on-one?!

It decided to show Darkrai that battles between legendary Pokémon were not something a mere "Ka-kaah" could interfere with!

"We're joining in too!" Johan declared.

"Gengar, Will-O-Wisp, Thunder Wave, Confuse Ray, Lick—use everything! Spam it all!!"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar smirked evilly.

With Darkrai and Palkia drawing attention at the front, it had nothing to worry about.

Sure, these moves might not deal much damage to Dialga, but they were—annoying.

At the same time,

Cynthia and Garchomp sprang into action.

"Garchomp, Dragon Rush!!"

Johan didn't stop her. Instead, he attempted to communicate with Palkia using his Aura powers.

[Palkia!]

No matter what, they had clearly shown they were helping it. It should at least acknowledge them, right?

If they could establish communication, or even cooperation, they might be able to lure the dragons away from Twinleaf Town and minimize the danger.

But Palkia ignored him completely.

After a while, it finally responded, seemingly annoyed.

[Get lost!!]

A mere human buzzing in its ear—how irritating!

Johan grinned.

You little…

"Garrr!!" (Too much bullying!!)

At that moment, Dialga had finally had enough.

It was supposed to be a one-on-one fight—so why did the enemy suddenly have backup?!

If you've ever been in a street fight, you know that when someone interferes, the anger is usually directed at the one who pulled a fast one.

So…

Hummm—

Dialga completely ignored Palkia.

The blue gem on its chest gleamed brilliantly.

Johan's expression changed. Forget Palkia's attitude—there was no time for that now!

"Dodge, quickly!"

Boom!!

A blinding blue beam tore through the air toward Darkrai.

In an instant, Darkrai was blasted away, crashing through countless buildings, leaving a trail of destruction across Twinleaf Town.

Seeing this, Palkia didn't hesitate.

It slashed its claws, unleashing several Spatial Rends toward Dialga.

But Dialga was now focused on one thing—taking Johan and his group down.

It had realized—Darkrai was following Johan's commands!

A mere human?!

Dragon Claw!

To the left, Spatial Rend created a distortion in space.

To the right, Roar of Time froze time itself.

Johan and Dragonite were trapped.

Johan's expression darkened as he took a deep breath and patted Dragonite.

It was time to go all out!

Johan and Dragonite had no space to dodge!

Seeing this, Johan's expression darkened. He took a deep breath and patted Dragonite, signaling it to prepare—this was going to be a fight for survival!

"Dragon Rush!!"

Just then, a familiar voice rang out.

From above, Garchomp, clad in its draconic aura, dove straight toward Dialga's incoming claw.

Cynthia's face was ice-cold, her sharp gaze filled with unwavering determination.

Boom!!

Dialga's claw was knocked off course.

"Garrr!!"

Dialga roared in pain.

It had momentarily forgotten about Garchomp—though it wasn't as much of a threat as Darkrai, it had been persistently buzzing around the battlefield.

"This girl…"

She really went all in when she fought.

Cynthia and Garchomp had successfully diverted Dialga's attention.

She even spared a glance toward Johan, offering him a gentle smile.

"Machamp, Dynamic Punch!!"

Below, Bruno's voice rang out.

It seemed he had arrived as well.

At the same time—

Palkia's attacks finally landed, explosions erupting around Dialga.

"Garrr—!!"

Dialga's eyes burned red.

It was furious!

Seeing Palkia attack, it naturally wasn't going to back down.

The two legendary dragons, wrapped in the powers of time and space, clashed again.

With each blow, Twinleaf Town crumbled further into ruin.

Whoosh—

Darkrai, battered and bruised, returned to the battlefield.

Seeing Dialga and Palkia about to unleash their signature attacks again, it didn't hesitate—immediately diving between them.

It hovered in the center.

In its mind, it recalled the scene Johan had "shown" it the night before.

[Alicia: "The garden… it belongs to everyone. You can stay here, Darkrai. Thank you…"]

Darkrai's gaze hardened. It roared:

"This—"

"Is everyone's garden!"

"This is not your battlefield! Get—"

"Out!!"

That small, shadowy figure bore the weight of the entire town's survival.

This was its mission.

Its destiny.

Hummmm—

Spacial Rend!

Roar of Time!

Time and space twisted violently.

With its small frame, Darkrai held up a dark veil to block the two devastating forces, preventing them from clashing and annihilating Twinleaf Town.

But…

Even as strong as Darkrai was, it couldn't hold back the power of two legendary dragons.

It was too much.

Struggling, Darkrai turned toward Johan.

"Please… protect the garden… I beg you!"

Johan's heart tensed. His brows furrowed.

"Tch."

Darkrai might be reckless…

But damn, was it badass.

"…Fine, fine." Johan shook his head. His frustration faded, replaced by a fierce determination.

Then let's go crazy together!

If there was ever a time to be reckless—

Why not now?!

"Gengar!!" Johan roared. "Mega Evolution!!"

"Ka-kaah!!"

A brilliant burst of rainbow-colored energy engulfed Gengar as it evolved into Mega Gengar.

Then, without hesitation, it lunged forward, ramming into Darkrai and knocking it out of the collision's epicenter.

BOOM!!

At that moment, Roar of Time and Spacial Rend collided once more—

The impact blasted Darkrai and Mega Gengar away.

Cynthia, Garchomp, and Bruno struggled against the shockwave, barely keeping their footing.

Darkrai, like a broken kite, crashed into the ground, its condition unknown.

Mega Gengar, though struggling, managed to steady itself in midair, positioning itself in front of Johan once again.

"Garrr!!"

"Draaaaa!!"

Even after that clash, Dialga and Palkia still weren't stopping.

But now, only a third of Twinleaf Town remained—

Johan could faintly hear the distant cries for help.

His heart grew heavier.

If this kept going…

Who knew how many people would die?

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar stuck out its tongue and let out a ferocious roar.

Finally, it caught the dragons' attention.

Dialga & Palkia: ?

One Darkrai was down, and now another one popped up?!

But this one was way weaker—no need to worry.

Gengar was furious.

They actually ignored him?!

"Cha!!"

Behind him, an excited shout rang out.

Johan's eyes lit up.

"Rotom, Teleport it over!"

Rotom: Σ(дlll)

"Dragonite, let's go!"

If not for protecting Johan, Dragonite would've joined the fight long ago.

Now, given the order, it roared with excitement and flapped its wings, soaring into battle.

Dragonite had never backed down from another dragon!

Seeing the dragons about to clash again, Gengar roared:

"Ka-kaah!!"

Hey, you two little dragons, come at me!!

It even curled a finger in challenge.

Dialga and Palkia froze.

Then exchanged glances.

This guy actually taunted them?!

Enraged, the two dragons prepared to attack—

Clink! Clink! Clink!

A clear metallic rattling sound rang out.

Hearing it, both Dialga and Palkia visibly stiffened.

They turned their heads—

And saw…

A Dragonite soaring through the sky, carrying a handsome young man—

Holding a crimson-red chain!

!!

"You two are way too cocky!" Johan bellowed, hurling the chain forward.

"That's…!" Cynthia gasped, her pupils shrinking. "The blood-red chain from ancient legends…"

"Garrr!!"

For the first time, Dialga and Palkia looked truly nervous.

As the chain unfurled midair, extending far beyond its original size—

The two dragons instantly tried to escape.

The chain could only bind one of them.

No way were they both going down!

"Garrr!!" Dialga roared in desperation.

Why is it always ME?!

The red chain shot toward Dialga, its mystical energy sealing the flow of time!

Seeing this, Palkia let out a sigh of relief.

Time to run!

In the rubble below, Darkrai struggled to rise, staring at the crimson glow in disbelief.

So this…

Was the power of Plan B?

Plan B?

If Johan had heard Darkrai's thoughts, he would've told it—

No. This was Plan C!

"You think you can run now? Too late!!"

Johan sneered.

"Gengar! You've been watching your 'virtual pet' for a while—time to let it out for a walk!"

"Ka-kaah!!"

Mega Gengar cackled.

Roar!!

A low, eerie growl rumbled from its body.

Dark mist poured from Mega Gengar's form—shaping into the silhouette of another dragon.

Sensing this, Dialga and Palkia both froze, their eyes widening in disbelief.

Giratina?!

Impossible!

Wasn't Giratina sealed in the Distortion World?!

Yes, Giratina couldn't leave.

But…

Just like how Dialga and Palkia's realms had intersected due to Arceus' influence—

Giratina's seal had also loosened.

It could project more of its power.

This was the one chance Johan and Giratina had been waiting for!

That spectral form of Giratina surged into Mega Gengar's body—making it swell even larger!

In an instant, it flashed in front of Palkia.

And then—

It punched Palkia in the face.

BOOM!!

At the same time, Johan leaped from Dragonite's back, plummeting straight down.

He shouted:

"Dragonite—Outrage!!"

"Draaaa!!!"

Dragonite's eyes burned red, a crimson aura raging around it.

Outrage Mode—ACTIVATED!!

"Again—Outrage!!"

Already in a frenzy, Dragonite unleashed a second Outrage mid-Outrage!

Double Outrage Mode—ACTIVATED!!

"Crush it!!"

Johan never let grudges slide.

That "Get lost" from Palkia earlier?

Yeah, he remembered.

"Draaaaa!!!"

Dragonite, wreathed in a deep red aura, shot toward Palkia like a crimson comet.

Boom!!

"Bruno!" Johan yelled.

"On it!"

Bruno, having received Johan's Aura message beforehand, had been waiting for this moment.

Without hesitation, he commanded his Pokémon to attack.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

Palkia, trying to escape, was already thrown off by the Red Chain and Giratina's projection.

Now, with Gengar's sucker punch, Dragonite's Outrage, and Bruno's team's coordinated assault—

Its body completely lost control, getting smashed into Dialga.

And at that moment—

The Red Chain tightened.

Sealing them both.

"Hmph!" Johan smirked. "Mere Twin Dragons."

Whoosh—

From above, Garchomp soared in an arc.

Cynthia, carrying Johan in a princess carry, caught him midair.

"Garrr—"

Garchomp snorted, turning its head.

You get a pass this time.

Johan, Cynthia, Mega Gengar, Dragonite, Garchomp, and the barely-standing Darkrai now hovered before the captured Twin Dragons.

Hummm—

Johan's Pokédex flashed, revealing Giratina's blood-red eyes.

"Garrr!" (I vote we kill them!)
