# 448: Just Call Me ‘Grain in the Field’ (BONUS)

The sun set beyond the western mountains.

Johan waited the entire afternoon, but the Iron Masked Marauder never showed up.

Did the timeline shift?

Or perhaps, for some other reason, the Marauder had not yet tracked down this era's Celebi?

If so, that was a good thing.

It saved them a lot of unnecessary trouble.

After all, the Iron Masked Marauder was not just one of Team Rocket's highest-ranking executives—

He was a truly evil man.

Team Rocket had people like Jessie, James, and Meowth, who pretended to be villains but in reality cared deeply for Pokémon.

And then—

There were people like the Iron Masked Marauder.

A man who embodied every negative trait of a villain:

Cruelty. Deception. Self-interest.

And above all—ambition.

Outwardly, he claimed to be seeking Celebi for Giovanni.

But in reality, he wanted to use Celebi to seize control of Team Rocket—

To overthrow Giovanni and conquer the world.

To achieve his goals, he would exploit and discard anything—

Even his own Pokémon.

Avoiding contact with him was undoubtedly the best course of action.

But Johan still had one lingering question.

—How did the Lake of Life become so polluted?

There was no clear answer.

All he could do now was help Celebi restore the lake.

The golden sunset bathed the vast forest in an orange glow.

In this warm twilight, Johan and his companions laid out a picnic blanket by the Lake of Life.

"Bii~ Bii~~"

Without the Lake of Life's power to aid its recovery, and after exhausting itself trying to purify the lake, Celebi had not yet fully regained its strength.

But thanks to Johan's painstaking generosity, constantly providing it with rare restoration items like Revival Herbs, Revive Shards, and Full Restores, Celebi's condition was far better than when it first arrived.

At this moment—

It was playing with Larvitar.

Larvitar was waving a carp streamer, wearing a little origami hat, and happily shaking the flag at the lake's edge.

It had received this flag from Mr. White back in the village.

While shaking it, Larvitar was also muttering something under its breath—

As if praying for the lake's restoration.

Celebi hovered midair, watching Larvitar's adorable actions, covering its mouth as it giggled.

That silver bell-like laughter seemed to make the surrounding air fresher.

Because Larvitar was still young, Johan didn't assign it any tasks.

It was simply keeping Celebi company.

The other Pokémon, however, were "working" under Johan's guidance—

Preparing dinner.

Since he hadn't planned to camp out in the wild, Johan hadn't packed much food.

His Pokémon's Pokéblock supply was well-stocked, but his own provisions were limited.

Still—

That wasn't a big problem.

In a forest this vast, finding food wasn't difficult.

For example—

Because it had rained earlier, Gengar had collected a bunch of mushrooms.

The only issue was—

Mushrooms could be poisonous.

For those unfamiliar, distinguishing edible from toxic mushrooms was nearly impossible.

So—

"Ka-kaah~ (っ)🎵"

Gengar, the 'Poison Tester,' stepped up.

It popped a mushroom into its mouth, chewing loudly.

After a few seconds—

"Ka-kaah!"

This one's safe!

These ones are poisonous!

Gengar used the most primitive and honest method possible—

Trial by eating.

In the end, all the poisonous mushrooms went straight into Gengar's mouth.

Leaving behind only a small pile of edible ones.

"Larvitar! Put down that Paras!"

"Yoji?"

Larvitar blinked innocently at Johan.

Then it looked at Gengar.

Then at the large, bright red mushroom in its hands.

It swallowed twice.

Reluctantly, it put the mushroom down.

Paras: (⚆_⚆)

"Good boy. Here, eat some soft shale instead."

"Yoji!!"

Excited, Larvitar cheered, crunched into a rock, and then ran off to keep playing with Celebi.

"Garruu~!"

Urshifu returned from hunting—its face brimming with excitement.

A huge harvest!

Nature's bounty had arrived!

In its hand—

Was a bewildered-looking Farfetch'd.

"…The wild Pokémon population here is pretty abundant. Even Farfetch'd…" Johan mused.

Wait.

Johan's expression suddenly mirrored the Farfetch'd.

You caught a Farfetch'd?

"Goka!"

Charcadet rolled up its sleeves.

Time to start cooking!

🔥🔥🔥

A burst of flames erupted.

Farfetch'd: Σ(дlll)

—It finally realized what was happening.

It was on the menu.

Johan massaged his temples.

"Let it go."

"Garruu?"

Urshifu looked confused.

Such a plump duck…

Wasn't Farfetch'd considered a Johto specialty dish?

Johan rubbed his forehead.

He honestly didn't expect them to actually catch a Farfetch'd.

It was practically on the verge of extinction.

Even the Pokémon League had passed laws to protect it…

"…Release it."

"Garruu."

Fine.

Urshifu nodded and let go.

Farfetch'd breathed a sigh of relief.

It was safe.

"…But leave the leek behind."

"Gaaah!! ~(TロT)σ"

Nightfall

Beneath the brilliant night sky, young Samuel Oak lay in the grass, patting his full belly, looking utterly satisfied.

"…I didn't expect you to be such a good cook, Johan…"

Johan sipped coffee, leaning against Dragonite's soft belly, absentmindedly stroking Ninetales' fur.

Meanwhile, Urshifu, still full of energy, was practicing punches by the riverside.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar played hide-and-seek with Tinkaton, Larvitar, and Celebi.

With an exaggerated jump-scare, it popped out, waving its giant red tongue.

The small Pokémon screamed and scattered.

Gengar cackled.

Larvitar panicked and leaped into Johan's arms.

Had Johan not trained his physical endurance recently, this "Rocket Headbutt" might have made him puke up dinner.

Unfortunately—

Larvitar's hiding skills were… terrible.

It buried its head in Johan's arms, but its little green tail still stuck out, twitching.

Celebi noticed.

Its eyes twinkled mischievously.

It dove behind Johan, clutching his back, peeking cautiously over his shoulder.

When it saw Gengar looking its way, it yelped and hid again.

Tinkaton, meanwhile, was rushing over with her massive hammer.

Johan's expression changed instantly.

"Tinkaton! Look, a shooting star!"

"Cha?"

Tinkaton paused, turning to the sky.

And—

A streak of light flashed across the heavens.

A real shooting star.

"Ka-kaah~~"

The Pokémon all looked up, mesmerized.

Johan also gazed at it, a certain mischievous, wish-granting Pokémon flashing through his mind.

"…Jirachi must be sleeping peacefully."

And so—

Under the starlit sky, Larvitar and Celebi curled up against Johan, and soon—

Fell asleep.

Johan smiled, pulling a blanket over them.

Oak, watching, suddenly spoke:

"…Johan."

"Hm?"

"If I said I could foresee the future… would you believe me?"

"…Huh?"

Johan smiled.

"In the future, when you grow old… you'll meet a boy named Ash."

"…Ash?"

Oak listened carefully.

"A boy with big dreams. And you… will guide him."

Samuel Oak listened carefully, his eyes filled with curiosity.

"Guide him?"

Johan nodded.

"He will be an important child. His greatest dream is to become a Pokémon Master."

Oak pondered for a moment before asking, "And how do I guide him?"

Johan smiled.

"When the time comes, he will come to you—to your lab in Pallet Town."

Oak tilted his head.

Pallet Town?

That place was just a tiny countryside village.

"His most important partner will be a Pikachu." Johan continued, "So no matter what happens, no matter how late he is, make sure to leave him that Pikachu."

Oak fell silent, processing the words carefully.

"…His most important partner?"

He lowered his head in thought.

Even though he did not yet know the details, Oak understood what Johan meant.

One day, he would meet a young boy with a Pikachu.

And he must ensure they meet.

After a long moment, Oak looked up—his gaze filled with determination.

"I understand."

Johan let out a soft sigh.

This butterfly effect… was getting out of control.

But at least he could try to correct or stabilize some things.

Although Oak still didn't know Johan's name,

This night—

He had seen many things.

He had learned about coexisting with Pokémon, respecting nature, and understanding the bond between trainers and their Pokémon.

To Oak, the young man before him didn't just seem like a fellow researcher.

He felt more like a mentor.

A teacher.

And Oak…

Respected him deeply.

After a long pause, Oak took a deep breath and stood up.

Then, he bowed slightly.

"Sir… I still don't know your name."

Johan hesitated.

Ah… finally, he's asking.

If he remained silent now, it would seem rude.

After a moment of thought, Johan answered,

"…Just call me 'Grain in the Field.'"

Oak blinked.

"…Grain in the Field?"

What kind of name is that?

Still, he engraved it into his memory.

Johan himself was a little surprised that he had blurted out those words.

Wait—

Wasn't 'Grain in the Field' the name that Professor Oak once used in a research paper?

Johan shook his head.

Best not to overthink time loops.

Thinking too much about causality and paradoxes would just give him a headache.

Instead, he smiled and changed the subject.

"Get some rest. We still have to restore the lake tomorrow."

Oak nodded and pulled out a simple blanket from his bag.

He lay down nearby, staring at the starry sky reflected in the lake.

A soft night breeze rippled across the water, carrying a peaceful silence.

Johan glanced at the two tiny creatures sleeping beside him.

Larvitar and Celebi had fallen asleep against each other, just like Pikachu and Celebi in the old Pokémon movie.

Chuckling, Johan gently draped a blanket over them.

Without the Lake of Life, Celebi's recovery would be much slower.

But maybe…

This would become a special experience for it.

"…Oowoo."

Ninetales curled up in Johan's lap, its fluffy tails spreading out like a blanket.

Gengar let out a long yawn, pulled out its Ghost Cloak, and floated midair before flopping down to sleep.

Behind them, Dragonite's breathing was deep and steady.

Tinkaton leaned against her massive hammer, already dozing off.

By the river, Urshifu continued training—practicing punches in the moonlight.

Meanwhile, Charcadet sat cross-legged in the center of camp, emitting a faint warm glow, keeping everyone cozy through the night.

Everything was calm.

The Next Morning

"Come on, drink it. It'll help you recover faster."

"…Bii."

Celebi eyed the cup of 'Revival Herb Tea' in Gengar's hands with pure dread.

It shook its head furiously.

No way. Absolutely not.

Johan sighed.

"Drink it, and I'll make you macarons later."

"Bii?!"

Celebi's eyes lit up instantly.

"…Really?"

"Of course."

Gengar handed over the cup of bitter tea, grinning.

Then, it clinked its cup against Celebi's.

'I'll drink first—you take your time.'

With that, Gengar chugged the entire cup.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Its body trembled violently.

POWER.

Celebi hesitated, then held its breath—

And downed the whole thing in one gulp.

"Bii!! (ಥ_ಥ)"

It was awful.

Before it could even process the bitterness, Johan immediately popped a sweet Pokéblock into its mouth.

"…Bii?"

Celebi blinked.

The sweetness quickly erased the bitterness.

Johan smiled, patting its head.

"See? Now it's not so bad."

Celebi chewed thoughtfully and realized—

He was right!

Excited, it zipped around Johan in circles.

"Bii~ Bii~!"

Back to Work

"Alright, everyone," Johan clapped his hands.

"Time to continue purifying the water."

"Bii~~!"

Celebi nodded enthusiastically.

"Got it!" Oak clenched his fist, full of determination.

Even the wild Pokémon had begun gathering again.

Unlike yesterday, today—

Everyone worked in perfect sync.

Celebi purified a portion of the lake.

Then, the Pokémon used barriers to keep the clean water separate from the polluted sections.

The progress was steady and smooth.

By afternoon, however—

Something strange happened.

"Ka-kaah!"

While swimming in the now-clear parts of the lake, Gengar seemed to discover something.

It dived underwater, and a moment later—

Resurfaced holding a metallic object.

It looked like a mechanical arm—

Half-buried beneath mud and algae.

"…What is this?" Oak frowned.

Johan narrowed his eyes.

Why was something like this buried in the Lake of Life?

"…Is this what polluted the water?"

"Keep searching," Johan ordered.

"See if there's more."

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar, Dragonite, and Urshifu dove into the water.

Soon—

More mechanical parts were pulled from the lakebed.

Johan turned to Rotom.

"Analyze them."

Rotom scanned the metallic debris.

"These appear to be… automated Pokémon-capturing devices! Roto!"

Johan's eyes darkened.

Capturing devices?

A horrifying possibility surfaced in his mind.

If these were hidden beneath the lake, then maybe—

Someone had been using the lake to trap Pokémon.

Wild Pokémon would come to drink water, only to be snatched by these machines.

And when they fought back, breaking the machines—

Toxic fuel and oil leaked into the water, polluting the lake.

Beep—Beep Beep—

Suddenly, one of the machines started flashing.

"It's still active! Roto!" Rotom exclaimed.

Johan's expression darkened.

If it was still transmitting data…

That meant whoever set these traps…

Knew they were being tampered with.

And then—

BOOM!

A loud rumbling echoed from deep in the forest.

Flocks of Pidgey and Pidgeotto took to the skies in panic.

Wild Pokémon fled in all directions.

Johan looked up—

A massive, spider-like machine emerged from the trees.

Atop the machine—

Stood a man wearing a golden mask.

His red vest covered the flaming red 'R' symbol on his chest.

Team Rocket's highest-ranking officer.

The Iron Masked Marauder.

And behind him—

A group of unknown individuals was approaching fast.

Johan sighed.

…So much for avoiding trouble.

"HAHAHAHAHA—"

The Marauder let out a wild, arrogant laugh.

"To think I'd find Celebi this easily!"

His burning gaze locked onto the tiny Pokémon behind Johan.

"Bii…"

He raised

a hand, grinning cruelly.

"Hand over Celebi."

"Do that, and maybe—just maybe— I'll let you live."

Johan squinted and took out his phone.

He pointed it at the Marauder.

"Say cheese."

Click!

"…Huh?"

The Marauder instinctively made a peace sign.

—Then immediately realized what he had done.

"You—!!"

Johan smirked and sent the photo to Matori.

Along with a message:

"I heard you've got connections in Johto. Can you take care of this problem for me?"

# 449: Team Rocket’s Reaction (BONUS)

Viridian City, Kanto – Team Rocket Headquarters.

A sharp ringing interrupted Giovanni's work, and a massive video call projection appeared on the wall.

"Boss, the Marauder seems to be picking a fight with Johan!" Matori said anxiously.

The Marauder?

Johan?

A fight?

Giovanni was slightly stunned—this was unexpected.

Within Team Rocket, his authority was absolute. Few would dare disobey his commands.

However, there were always exceptions—like the Marauder.

Unlike most Team Rocket members whom Giovanni personally recruited and trained, the Marauder had joined halfway through. He was originally a Pokémon hunter.

But Giovanni never cared much about backgrounds. He knew the Marauder was ambitious, but so what? Ambition meant nothing in front of him.

Still, ever since Team Rocket began its recent reforms, the Marauder had become increasingly vocal in his opposition.

After all, as a former Pokémon hunter, he had joined Team Rocket to make capturing and selling Pokémon easier. Now, with the organization undergoing reform, his profits had been cut in half.

Giovanni had been keeping an eye on him, but since the Marauder remained outwardly obedient and was useful in eliminating rogue Pokémon hunters, Giovanni hadn't bothered acting against him.

But now, he had clashed with Johan.

"Have you called him?" Giovanni asked, fingers interlaced, his tone calm.

"No one's picking up," Matori said helplessly.

That was why she was so anxious.

She knew exactly what kind of person the Marauder was.

Giovanni chuckled. "It seems he's found a Pokémon that gives him confidence."

"What should we do, Boss?"

"You don't trust Johan's strength?"

The Marauder had earned his high-ranking position in Team Rocket due to his power.

But Johan was no longer the person they first met—he had even gone toe-to-toe with the rulers of time and space.

"That's not it," Matori sighed, massaging her temples. "I just think Johan might… be really angry."

"Oh?"

"Johan despises Pokémon hunters, and the Marauder has always acted like one. Will this conflict cause Johan to take his anger out on Team Rocket?"

Matori had a good understanding of Johan.

If Johan weren't angry, he wouldn't have sent her that photo.

After all, their relationship with Johan had never been openly discussed.

There was a silent understanding between them.

"More importantly…"

Matori hesitated before continuing in a lower voice, "I just got word that the Marauder secretly took all the failed 'Dark Balls.' If one of Johan's Pokémon gets forcibly captured, or if Johan himself gets hurt…"

Dark Balls were one of Team Rocket's inventions.

They could forcibly capture Pokémon, ignoring their will and any resistance—even if the Pokémon was already owned by a Trainer.

However, Dark Balls had extreme side effects. Captured Pokémon lost their sense of self and became uncontrollably violent.

While they temporarily increased a Pokémon's power, it came at the cost of destroying the Pokémon's future.

Originally, Team Rocket had developed Dark Balls for Mewtwo, and secondarily to strengthen Team Rocket's forces.

But after Johan pacified Mewtwo and Giovanni decided to reform the organization, research on Dark Balls was halted.

Only a batch of defective ones remained in storage.

Giovanni tapped his fingers on the desk.

"He wouldn't take it out on us—Johan isn't that petty."

He paused, then continued, "But… that possibility isn't zero."

His eyes narrowed slightly as he stroked Persian.

Sensing Giovanni's mood, Persian slowly stood up, baring its fangs.

But Giovanni simply smiled and patted Persian's head, calming it down.

Then, he said, "Send Archer and the others. All four of them."

Matori's body tensed.

"A-All four?" she asked in shock.

Those were the Four Executives of Team Rocket's Johto branch—equivalent in rank to Kanto's Sabrina, Koga, and Lt. Surge.

They essentially managed half of Team Rocket's Johto operations.

She had expected Giovanni to send one or two at most, but all four?

She instinctively shrank back.

The Boss really valued Johan.

But wouldn't this make the Pokémon League nervous?

Giovanni's smile widened. "Didn't you say Johan might misunderstand us? Then let's show him our commitment!"

"And… we'll deal with this little problem once and for all."

"Yes, sir!"

—

Goldenrod City, Johto – Team Rocket's Secret Base.

A man in a white suit, with short pale-blue hair, stood in the room. On his chest was the crimson "R" of Team Rocket.

Archer, leader of the Four Executives.

He folded one arm behind his back and touched his chin with the other, lightly coughing to gather the attention of the room.

There were three others present.

A red-haired woman in a white Team Rocket uniform sat by the bar—Ariana.

A man in a black uniform, wearing a black cap, leaned against the wall in the shadows—Proton.

A middle-aged man with a mohawk of purple hair lounged lazily on the couch—Petrel.

These four were the Johto branch's highest-ranking Executives.

Archer spoke with a serious expression.

"The Boss's orders: support the 'Advisor' and eliminate the Marauder."

"The 'Advisor'?" Ariana raised an eyebrow.

She had long heard that Team Rocket had a new highest-ranking officer, codenamed "Advisor," whom Giovanni valued highly.

Yet, she had never met him.

Even at last year's Team Rocket annual gathering, he was absent.

Rumor had it that the organization's internal reforms were largely influenced by this mysterious figure, though there was no concrete proof.

And now, the fact that all four Executives were being sent suggested it was true.

Proton chuckled, adjusting his cap. "I knew the Marauder was reckless, but provoking the 'Advisor'? That's a new level of stupidity."

"Ahh…"

Petrel stretched lazily on the couch. "If the Boss says go, we go."

Archer was satisfied with their reactions.

Team Rocket was Giovanni's Team Rocket. He had never liked the Marauder, who constantly challenged Giovanni's authority.

"If all four of us go, what about the League?" Ariana asked.

At this, Proton and Petrel smirked.

Archer chuckled as well.

"Well… let's give the League something to worry about."

Indeed, the Johto League quickly became tense.

An emergency meeting of all Gym Leaders was held.

What was Team Rocket planning?

Meanwhile, Johan sighed as he watched the Marauder take out his phone—only to put it away again.

It seemed Matori's "influence" wasn't working anymore.

Understandable.

The Marauder likely believed that since he had found Celebi, capturing it would allow him to surpass Giovanni and take control of Team Rocket.

At that point, why bother answering a call from Giovanni's secretary?

"Tch…"

The Marauder gritted his teeth and pulled out a small black sphere from his vest.

As he pressed the button, the ball expanded into a pitch-black Poké Ball, crackling with energy.

A Dark Ball!

With his escape routes cut off, the Marauder had no choice. If he failed to capture Celebi today, he'd have to flee into the mountains and return to being a rogue Pokémon hunter.

He tossed the Dark Ball.

A Tyranitar emerged from the gray mist, its gaze sharp and its aura ferocious.

"Yoji!"

A small Larvitar chirped excitedly—wasn't this its final evolution?

But instead of responding, Tyranitar roared and stomped its foot.

Earthquake!

The ground split as a shockwave hurtled toward Larvitar.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar appeared in an instant, grabbing Larvitar and dodging the attack.

The battle had begun.

And as the Marauder laughed maniacally, black Dark Balls fired from a mechanical arm, forcefully capturing every wild Pokémon in sight.

But Johan's Pokémon stepped forward.

"Ninetales, Oowoo~"

"Tinkaton, Cha!"

"Urshifu, Garruu!"

"Dragonite, Draaa!"

The fight was on.

The Marauder smirked and threw a Dark Ball at Gengar.

But Gengar simply ate it.

# 450: Dark Balls? Just Trash.

Ate it…?

The Marauder stood frozen in shock, staring at Gengar, who had just licked its lips after swallowing the Dark Ball whole.

This was the first time he had encountered something like this.

The Dark Ball—an unbeatable tool capable of capturing any Pokémon, wild or already owned—had just been eaten.

No reaction at all?

Even if he had thrown it into water, it would've at least made a splash.

Unwilling to believe it, he threw another Dark Ball at Gengar.

"Ka-kaah!"

Once again, Gengar opened its mouth and gulped the ball down.

The Marauder: "..."

Was this even allowed?

He was seriously beginning to question how Johan had captured this Gengar. It completely disregarded the basic laws of Pokémon battles!

And that ability to mass-hypnotize wild Pokémon—since when could Gengar even do that?

But if he couldn't forcibly capture Gengar, he had no choice but to keep fighting.

Celebi was a Pokémon he had to catch!

"Tyranitar, kick up a Sandstorm!"

The Marauder abandoned the idea of capturing Gengar and switched to brute force.

"Tyrannn!!"

With a roar, Tyranitar summoned a swirling storm of sand around itself.

At the same time, his Scizor, Excadrill, and other Pokémon sprang into action.

"Oowoo~~"

Ninetales stepped forward gracefully, snow-white powder drifting from its fur. The snowfall, which had already started, thickened under its control.

The battle of weather began—howling wind and snow against raging sand.

The Marauder finally realized how troublesome Johan was.

His Tyranitar had been enhanced by a Dark Ball. Yet, despite striking first with its Sandstorm, it wasn't overwhelming Ninetales' snow. Instead, it was beginning to be countered.

In raw power, his Pokémon couldn't match Johan's.

But he had an advantage.

Sheer numbers.

"All of you, whip up a Sandstorm!"

The Dark Ball-enhanced wild Pokémon—including Sandslash, Nidoking, Dugtrio, and Rhyperior—roared in response.

"Garrr!!"

The swirling sands surged even higher, intensifying the storm.

"Yoji!!"

Larvitar finally realized the opposing Pokémon were enemies. It stomped its foot and roared, though its small, high-pitched voice lacked any real intimidation.

"Oowoo~~!!"

Ninetales' expression grew more serious. Against this many Pokémon using Sandstorm at once, its Blizzard was at risk of being suppressed.

The Marauder smirked, sensing the tide turning in his favor.

"Hyper Beam! All of you!"

A hum filled the air.

Nidoking, Nidoqueen, Ursaring, and several other powerful wild Pokémon opened their mouths, energy gathering.

But Johan's Pokémon weren't just standing still.

"Urshifu, Surging Strikes!"

"Tinkaton, Gigaton Hammer!"

"Dragonite, Dragon Rush!"

"Charcadet, Ninetales, Help Hand!"

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu charged forward, punching the ground so hard it cratered beneath its feet. Ignoring the raging Sandstorm and charging Hyper Beams, it rushed straight at Tyranitar.

A soft glow emitted from Charcadet, sending energy surging into Urshifu.

Tyranitar roared and slammed its claws down, sending jagged stone spikes shooting up from the ground to intercept Urshifu.

But Urshifu danced between them effortlessly, moving like flowing water before landing a devastating punch square on Tyranitar's face.

BAM!!

A follow-up kick. A sweeping punch.

Tyranitar was sent flying.

"Close Combat!"

Without hesitation, Urshifu pressed forward, launching a flurry of strikes. Tyranitar could only roar in pain, unable to resist.

In the end—

A powerful spinning kick sent Tyranitar crashing to the ground, creating a massive crater.

Meanwhile, battles raged on all sides.

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite dive-bombed from above, targeting the Marauder himself—only for Scizor to intercept.

BOOM!!

A deafening crash shook the battlefield. Dragonite was halted midair, but Scizor was sent flying like a cannonball.

"Cha!!"

Tinkaton spun its massive hammer, gaining momentum with each rotation. The air pressure alone was enough to sting.

When the time was right, it let the hammer fly—

BOOM BOOM BOOM!!

Like a scythe cutting through grass, wild Pokémon were sent sprawling.

The Marauder's Weezing tried to block it, but sensing the incoming force, it wisely floated away, letting the hammer pass.

CRASH!!

The battlefield was devastated.

Seconds later, silence fell.

The Marauder's breathing hitched.

But he wasn't done.

"Weavile!"

A shadow flitted behind Johan, Weavile's claws poised to strike his throat.

"Sir, watch out!" Professor Oak's grandson, Gary, shouted.

Johan didn't move.

Got him!

A victorious gleam flickered in the Marauder's eyes—only to vanish the next second.

CRACK!!

Weavile's claws stopped inches from Johan's throat, blocked by an invisible barrier.

And behind that barrier—was a thin layer of ice.

Johan caught Ninetales' warning glance out of the corner of his eye.

"Weavile?"

BOOM!!

A blast of ghostly blue fire slammed into Weavile.

Charcadet's eyes burned with determination.

Simultaneously, a beam of pale moonlight followed, striking Weavile midair.

"Below." Johan's voice was calm.

"Oowoo!"

Ninetales exhaled a breath of freezing air, sealing the ground beneath Johan in thick ice.

Excadrill, which had been burrowing up for a sneak attack, found itself frozen in place.

Waiting for it—

"Goka!!"

Charcadet's fists glowed red-hot as it punched Excadrill, flames surging through the tunnels.

The Marauder cursed.

"Useless!"

But before he could finish, a chill ran down his spine.

Something was behind him.

Sweat dripped down his back as he turned his head—

Gengar grinned at him.

"Ka-kaah!"

The Marauder's pupils shrank.

But his machine wasn't useless—it was Team Rocket's top technology, modified for him.

Just as Gengar reached for him, barriers of Light Screen and Reflect activated around the cockpit.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar prodded the barrier, intrigued.

The Marauder let out a breath. Not many Pokémon could sneak up on him.

But then—Gengar chuckled.

With a swipe, a dimensional rift appeared.

And through it, Gengar's hand emerged—inside the barrier.

Holding a Shadow Ball.

The Marauder paled.

BOOM!!

The machine exploded, black liquid seeping into the Lake of Life.

Pollution spread.

Celebi, seeing the corruption worsen, panicked.

"Bi!!"

It flew from Johan's arms, glowing green, trying to purify the lake—

But this time, the pollution resisted.

Johan's eyes darkened.

The Dark Ball's effects had seeped into the poison.

"Attack Celebi!"

The Marauder threw more Dark Balls.

"Celebi!"

"Gengar!"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar dropped Weavile, appearing in front of Celebi, swallowing the Dark Balls whole.

Meanwhile, the Marauder's reinforcements arrived.

"Boss, the kid who stole from us ran!"

Johan's eyes narrowed.

Silver?

So Silver had already clashed with them?

Giovanni had worked so hard to reform Team Rocket for Silver's sake—yet the Marauder had just made him hate them more.

You really deserve to die.

But first—

"Ninetales, freeze the lake!"

"Oowoo~~"

A blizzard sealed the black liquid.

Time to finish this.

The Marauder and his men were swiftly overwhelmed.

Bound by vines, they lay defeated.

Johan glanced at the Dark Balls in his hands.

Then—

CRACK!

He crushed them underfoot.

The Marauder's eyes widened in horror.

"Dark Balls? Just trash."

"You!"

The Marauder's eyes widened in rage.

"Yoji!!"

Larvitar, still furious, stomped over and slapped the Marauder across the face.

Hands on its hips, it glared at him.

Look at what you've done to the forest! And you still dare complain?!

Johan's actions signaled the rest of his Pokémon. Gengar and the others promptly destroyed every remaining Dark Ball.

Immediately, the wild Pokémon and those under the Marauder's command were freed.

Seeing this, Gary Oak let out a breath of relief. Yet, a hint of guilt flickered across his face.

He had doubted his teacher.

To Johan, the Dark Balls…

Their design was interesting—a sort of "cheat code" in reality—but their flaws were too severe.

More importantly, Pokémon captured this way would never form a true bond with their Trainers. In a world where Mega Evolution, Dynamax, and Terastallization were becoming mainstream, such Pokémon would never reach their full potential.

Unless they could forcibly capture Legendaries…

But clearly, Dark Balls weren't capable of that.

So—

Dark Balls?

Just trash.

"T-Turn me in to the Pokémon League!"

The Marauder, now sporting a swollen cheek from Larvitar's slap, suddenly changed his tune.

Johan scoffed.

"Nice try."

He turned to Gengar.

"Wake them up."

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar let out a sinister chuckle and snapped its fingers.

The previously hypnotized wild Pokémon slowly stirred.

Without the Dark Balls suppressing their wills, their eyes filled with rage.

And they all turned toward the Marauder.

"N-No! Stay back!!"

Johan smirked.

"Relax, you won't die."

Death would be too troublesome to explain.

The Marauder's gaze darkened with resentment.

As long as he survived—as long as he lived through this—

The League couldn't hold him forever.

He still had hidden resources, caches of funds, and the knowledge of Dark Ball technology.

He could rebuild. He could rise again.

But before he could sink any deeper into his thoughts—

"Oh, right."

Johan's voice snapped him back to reality.

With a mocking smirk, Johan casually added:

"Your 'family' should be here soon to pick you up."
